TL;DR: 本文提出ACE框架，用于MoE大语言模型中的token自适应专家跳过。ACE包含全局谱代理（GSP）和路由器条件细化（RCR）两个互补组件，无需训练、校准或修改检查点，通过双重视角评估专家贡献并仅在两个视角都认为其低贡献时才跳过该专家。大量实验表明ACE在三个MoE模型和八个基准上优于现有动静基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构固定top-k路由会对每个token激活相同数量的专家槽位，导致大量冗余计算。已有专家跳过方法依赖路由置信度、校准数据或额外训练，无法可靠估计被路由专家的实际贡献，因此需要一种无需训练、无需校准且保留检查点的方法来准确评估专家贡献。

Method: ACE包含两个互补组件：1）全局谱代理（GSP），从耦合的gate、up、down投影及RMSNorm缩放中估计全局变换能力；2）路由器条件细化（RCR），从居中的路由器权重构专家特定方向原型，并沿路由偏好方向评估专家响应。推理时结合运行时路由门控，仅当两个视角都判定某专家槽位为低贡献时才跳过，始终保留top-1专家。所有专家统计离线计算，在线仅需查表和轻量标量运算。

Result: 在三个MoE大模型和八个基准上的广泛实验表明，ACE持续优于现有静态和动态基线方法，在激进专家跳过下优势愈发明显。例如在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上以50%跳过率时，ACE将WikiText-2困惑度降低7.96%，并将平均下游准确率比最强竞争方法提高4.15个百分点。

Conclusion: ACE通过训练免费、校准免费且保留检查点的双视角方法有效解决了MoE专家跳过中贡献估计不可靠的问题，实现了高效且准确的token自适应专家裁剪，为MoE推理效率优化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures provide an efficient paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs), yet fixed top-k routing activates the same number of expert slots for every token, causing substantial redundant computation. Existing expert-skipping methods often rely on router confidence, calibration data, or additional training, and therefore cannot reliably estimate the actual contribution of routed experts. To this end, we propose ACE, a training-free, calibration-free, and checkpoint-preserving framework for token-adaptive expert skipping in MoE-based LLMs. ACE contains two complementary components: 1) Global Spectral Proxy (GSP), which estimates global transformation capacity from the coupled gate, up, and down projections together with RMSNorm scaling; and 2) Router-Conditioned Refinement (RCR), which constructs expert-specific direction prototypes from centered router weights and evaluates expert responses along routing-preferred directions. During inference, ACE combines both estimates with runtime router gates and skips an expert slot only when both views identify it as low-contribution, while always retaining the top-1 expert. All expert statistics are computed offline, leaving only table lookups and lightweight scalar operations online. Extensive experiments across three MoE-based LLMs and eight benchmarks demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing static and dynamic baselines, with increasingly pronounced advantages under aggressive expert skipping. For instance, at a 50% skipping ratio on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, ACE reduces WikiText-2 perplexity by 7.96% and improves average downstream accuracy by 4.15 percentage points over the strongest competing method.

</details>


### [85] [Substrate-Aware AI Agents: Execution Context as a First-Class Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05232)
*Manu Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"基底盲视"（substrate blindness）概念，指AI智能体在规划时缺乏执行环境约束（如内存、时间、算力等）的感知。作者通过数值代码生成任务验证该命题：当向前沿大模型（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-Sol、Gemini 3.7 Flash）披露128MB内存和10秒墙钟时间的执行合约后，生成代码的峰值进程内存在14个可执行对照中有13个降低，平均墙钟时间在三组中全部下降（最快快3.1倍）；在96MB更紧合约下，合约披露组的正确且达标比例显著高于仅任务组，MaxRSS和墙钟时间分别降低49-74%和35-64%。结果表明最小执行合约能诱导代码发生结构性适应（如分块阻塞、float32保留、上三角遍历、原地/内存映射缓冲），建立了一个受控的基底感知智能体规划的概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在真实环境中选择动作时，其适用性取决于底层执行环境（内存、运行时、计算、操作约束），但当前智能体的规划状态中普遍缺失这类执行上下文信息，作者将这一缺失称为"基底盲视"。作者希望验证：将执行合约（如内存和时间的硬性限制）显式提供给模型，能否诱导其产生结构性代码更改以适配环境约束，从而为基底感知的智能体规划提供受控概念验证。

Method: 以高维成对欧氏距离计算的数值代码生成作为实验环境，选取三个前沿模型配置（Anthropic Claude Opus 5、OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol、Google Gemini 3.7 Flash），分别从两种条件下生成代码：（1）仅有任务描述；（2）附带128MB RAM和10.0秒墙钟时间的执行合约。随后对标称128MB和更紧的96MB两种合约下的代码进行可执行性、正确性和资源消耗审计，对比两组在峰值进程内存（MaxRSS）、墙钟时间和结构性代码特征上的差异。

Result: 在128MB合约下，合约披露在14个可执行的索引对齐对照中使13个降低峰值进程内存，三组模型平均墙钟时间全部降低，执行最快加速3.1倍。代码审计显示合约披露诱导了结构性更改：有界分块、float32精度保留、上三角矩阵遍历优化以及原地/内存映射缓冲。在96MB更紧合约下，独立采样的合约披露组达成正确且预算内结果的比例为Claude 4/5、GPT 5/5、Gemini 3/5，而仅任务组仅0/5、1/5、0/5；合约披露组的MaxRSS和墙钟时间较仅任务参考组分别降低49-74%和35-64%。

Conclusion: 研究建立了基底感知智能体规划的受控概念验证：一个最小的执行合约（内存与时间约束）能在代码生成前推动模型进行主动的结构性适应，将计算从无约束分配中迁移出来，并在执行前显著改善可观测的资源-时间曲线。这说明将执行环境上下文纳入智能体的规划基底，是提升其在实际运行约束下表现的有效且可控手段。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly select actions in environments whose memory, execution-time, runtime, compute, and operational constraints determine what counts as a suitable plan. We call the absence of this execution context from an agent's planning state substrate blindness. We test this general proposition through numerical code generation, where selected implementation choices and operational consequences are directly observable. Three frontier model configurations--Anthropic Claude Opus 5, OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol, and Google Gemini 3.7 Flash--generate code for a high-dimensional pairwise Euclidean-distance task either from the task alone or with a 128 MB RAM and 10.0 s wall-time contract. Contract disclosure reduced measured peak process memory in 13 of 14 executable index-aligned task-only versus contract-disclosed comparisons and reduced mean wall time in all three cohorts, making execution up to 3.1x faster. Across the audited corpus, disclosure produced structural code changes including bounded blocking, float32 retention, upper-triangle traversal, and in-place or memory-mapped buffers. At a tighter 96 MB contract, independently sampled contract-disclosed cohorts achieved correct-and-within-budget outcomes of 4/5 for Claude Opus 5, 5/5 for GPT-5.6-Sol, and 3/5 for Gemini 3.7 Flash, compared with task-only outcomes of 0/5, 1/5, and 0/5; cohort mean MaxRSS and wall time were 49-74% and 35-64% lower than their task-only references. These results establish a controlled proof of concept for substrate-aware agent planning: a minimal execution contract induces proactive structural adaptation in generated programs, shifting computation away from unconstrained allocations and substantially improving observed resource-time profiles before execution.

</details>


### [86] [Uncensored Open-weight Models: Redistribution as the Persistence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05241)
*10a Labs,:,Juliette Garcia,Hailey May,Bobby McKenzie,David Pham,Matthew Swain,Joshua Valdez,Corie Wieland,Zachary Yahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文剖析了一个快速扩张的生态系统，该生态系统正从开源权重AI模型中移除内置安全护栏。研究识别出关键生产者、下游复制品及新兴应用，发现2024年1月至2026年3月间HuggingFace上存在3,471个原始无审查模型，平均被重新打包2.4次；三个主要参与者占8,164次压缩再分发总量的52%。这些模型经量化并镜像到不同账户、格式和Ollama等注册表后，即使上游被删除仍可存续且更易在下游部署。在1,643个集成无审查大语言模型的GitHub应用中，25%被归类为明确恶意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源权重AI模型的普及，移除内置安全护栏（即"无审查"）的做法正在快速蔓延，形成一个值得关注的生态系统。作者旨在系统性地勾勒这一生态的全貌，包括其生产、再分发和应用层面的特征，以便理解其规模、结构与潜在风险。

Method: 作者通过系统性数据采集与分析对该生态进行画像：在HuggingFace平台上（2024年1月至2026年3月期间）识别3,471个原始无审查模型，跟踪其重复打包与压缩再分发现象；同时在GitHub上识别1,643个集成无审查大语言模型的应用，并对应用性质进行分类研判。

Result: 研究发现：3,471个原始无审查模型被平均打包2.4次；三个关键参与者主导了8,164次压缩再分发中的52%。模型的量化与跨账户镜像使其具有韧性，上游删除后依然存续且更易部署。GitHub上1,643个相关应用中，25%被分类为明确恶意。

Conclusion: 无审查AI模型生态具有集中化生产、高韧性传播和恶意应用丛生的特征，表明仅靠上游删源难以有效治理，需要针对再分发链条和下游应用采取更综合的应对策略。

Abstract: A rapidly expanding ecosystem of actors is removing built-in safety guardrails from open-weight AI models. We profile this ecosystem by identifying key producers, downstream reproductions, and emerging applications. Between January 2024 and March 2026, we identified 3,471 original uncensored models on HuggingFace, each repackaged an average of 2.4 times; three actors account for 52% of all 8,164 compressed redistributions. Once quantized and mirrored across separate accounts, formats, and registries such as Ollama, these models persist regardless of upstream removal and become easier to deploy downstream. Of the 1,643 identified GitHub applications integrating uncensored large language models (ULLMs), 25% were classified as explicitly malicious.

</details>


### [87] [Do LLMs Exhibit Coherent Knowledge Structures in Mathematical Reasoning? A Perspective from Knowledge Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05245)
*Peng Cui,Heejin Do,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于知识空间理论（KST）提出一个评估LLM在数学推理中知识结构的框架，通过对比八种开源和闭源LLM与真实人类学习者，发现LLM不遵循人类知识结构——频繁违反知识依赖关系、无法利用上下文相关知识提升表现，且LLM之间也缺乏一致的知识结构。这些结构性缺陷在基于准确率和LLM-as-judge的评估中不可见，为LLM知识缺乏人类式结构提供了行为证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类知识本质上是结构化且相互依赖的：掌握一个概念需要先掌握其前置概念。虽然LLM在复杂推理任务上表现优异，但其是否具备连贯的、类似人类的知识结构仍不清楚。作者希望引入KST作为规范性框架来分析LLM行为是否遵循原则性的知识依赖关系。

Method: 采用KST-grounded框架评估LLM在数学推理中的知识结构。具体评估了八种开源和闭源LLM，并与真实人类学习者进行对比分析。通过考察LLM是否遵守知识依赖关系（即前置知识问题应该答对才能答对依赖知识问题）以及是否能在上下文中利用相关知识改善依赖问题的表现来衡量其知识结构。

Result: 研究发现：(1) LLM不遵循人类知识结构——它们经常违反知识依赖关系，且未能利用上下文中提供的相关知识来提升依赖题目的表现；(2) 不同LLM之间也没有一致的知识结构，其知识分布的重复率较低。此外，这些结构缺陷在基于准确率和LLM-as-judge的评估中基本不可见。

Conclusion: 实验结果提供了行为层面的证据，表明当前LLM的知识并不遵循人类式的结构。即使LLM在准确率等传统指标上表现良好，其内部知识组织方式与人类存在根本性差异，且现有评估方法无法捕捉到这些结构性缺陷。

Abstract: Human knowledge is inherently structured and interdependent: mastery of a concept requires prior mastery of its prerequisites, a principle formalized by Knowledge Space Theory (KST). While LLMs achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they exhibit coherent, human-like knowledge structure. We introduce a KST-grounded framework for evaluating LLM knowledge structure in mathematical reasoning, using it as a normative framework to analyze whether LLM behavior adheres to principled knowledge dependencies. Evaluating eight open- and closed-source LLMs against real human learners, we find that (1) LLMs do not adhere to human knowledge structure -- they frequently violate knowledge dependencies and fail to leverage related knowledge provided in context to improve performance on dependent questions; (2) LLMs do not share a consistent knowledge structure among themselves, as reflected by low overlap in their knowledge distributions. Furthermore, these structural deficiencies remain largely invisible to accuracy-based and LLM-as-judge evaluations. Together, our results provide behavioral evidence that current LLMs knowledge does not follow a human-like structure.

</details>


### [88] [A Unified Physics-Aware Quantum Machine Learning Framework across Power GaN HEMTs and Logic Nanowire FETs: Predicting Unseen Process Splits and Held-Out Geometry Combinations with Lower Error and Tighter Split-to-Split Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05251)
*Rushat Rai,Yun-Yuan Wang,Autsada Kakaen,Pei-Jie Chang,Doan Viet Nguyen,Yuan-Chieh Chiu,Doldet Tantraviwat,Niall Tumilty,Simon See,Wen-Jay Lee,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Tian-Li Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的强化学习(RL)框架，用于发现紧凑的参数化量子电路(PQC)作为数据稀缺器件建模的替代模型。该框架在11个目标上均优于六种经典基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺的半导体器件建模场景中，经典机器学习模型的泛化性能和物理一致性有限，需要寻找一种紧凑且具备低分布外(OOD)误差的替代建模方法。

Method: 采用图神经网络(GNN)作为策略网络，通过近端策略优化(PPO)算法搜索电路架构，以留一组交叉验证(LOGOCV)在held-out过程或几何组上的误差作为奖励信号，在无显式物理约束和罚项的条件下训练RL策略。

Result: 在所有11个目标上取得了最低MAE，优于六种经典基线。在HEMT上，Ioff误差降低59%，VTH折叠变异性收窄81%；在NWFET上，VTH/SS/Ioff误差降低84%，Ioff折叠变异性收窄82%。

Conclusion: RL选择并由经典模拟的紧凑PQC在两种评估的器件数据集上表现出低OOD误差和改进的物理一致性，即使未施加任何显式物理约束、罚项或设备特定方程，也展现了其作为紧凑替代模型的潜力。

Abstract: We present a unified reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that discovers compact parametrized quantum circuits (PQCs) for data-scarce device modeling. A graph neural network (GNN) policy optimized by proximal policy optimization (PPO) searches circuit architectures using leave-one-group-out cross-validation (LOGOCV) error on held-out process or geometry groups as the reward. The framework achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) on all 11 targets versus six classical baselines, with 59% lower error (Ioff) and 81% tighter fold variability (VTH) for HEMTs and 84% lower error (VTH, SS, Ioff) and 82% tighter fold variability (Ioff) for NWFETs. These results demonstrate the potential of RL-selected, classically simulated PQCs as compact surrogates with low OOD error and improved physical consistency, despite imposing no explicit physical constraints, penalty terms, or device-specific equations, on the two evaluated device datasets.

</details>


### [89] [Commonsense Reasoning in Computer Vision: Foundations, Recent Advancements, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05257)
*Bahar Uddin Mahmud,Sumit Barua,Guan Yue Hong,Ajay Gupta,Hexu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了将常识知识整合到计算机视觉任务中的最新进展，系统总结了基于知识图谱、场景图、神经符号模型和常识增强变换器的方法，并指出了数据集偏差、知识不完整和集成挑战等当前局限性，最后展望了跨模态推理、可扩展常识知识注入和神经符号混合架构等未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常识推理对于计算机视觉理解日常场景至关重要，而传统基于CNN的视觉模型仅能识别图像中的特定物体，缺乏对物体间关系、动作和上下文因素的整体性理解。因此需要系统梳理如何通过整合常识知识来提升视觉模型的场景理解能力和空间推理能力。

Method: 系统性地综述了四类常识知识集成方法：基于知识图谱的方法、基于场景图的方法、神经符号模型以及常识增强的Transformer架构；同时分析各自的优缺点、当前局限和未来方向。

Result: 总结出当前领域面临的主要挑战包括数据集偏差、知识不完整性和集成困难，并识别出跨模态推理、可扩展常识知识注入和神经符号混合架构三大值得发展的研究方向。

Conclusion: 常识知识集成是推动视觉系统从单纯目标识别走向真正智能场景理解的关键路径。未来的开发需要克服现有方法在实际集成方面的问题，探索更具扩展性和跨模态的架构，以实现真正具备常识推理能力的智能视觉系统。

Abstract: Commonsense reasoning in computer vision encompasses integrating visual data and contextual knowledge, crucial for enhancing AI's understanding of everyday scenarios. This understanding not only improves machine learning models but also enhances their ability to interact meaningfully with humans and the environment. Unlike CNN-based conventional vision models, which are designed to identify objects within a specific image, incorporating commonsense knowledge enables models to interpret scenes in a more holistic manner, thereby improving their spatial ability to reason about relationships among objects and actions. This integration not only enhances object recognition but also facilitates a deeper understanding of the contextual factors, ultimately leading to more precise predictions and interactions in real-world applications. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of recent developments that integrate commonsense knowledge into computer vision tasks. We systematically review approaches based on knowledge graphs, scene graphs, neuro-symbolic models, and commonsense-augmented transformers. We also outline current limitations related to dataset bias, knowledge incompleteness, and integration challenges. Finally, we highlight prospective research trajectories in cross-modal reasoning, scalable commonsense knowledge injection, and neuro-symbolic hybrid architectures to develop truly intelligent visual systems.

</details>


### [90] [Trace2Tower: Transition-Aware EigenTrace Induction of Multi-Level Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05261)
*Jiazheng Sun,Boyu Yang,Binhao Yuan,Mingxuan Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Tower是一个过渡感知的EigenTrace框架，将原始轨迹蒸馏为稳健的技能层级。它通过将步级交互抽象为规范事件、构建统一图、进行对比谱分解来提取稳定且成功率高的行为模式，并动态生成技能塔。在ALFWorld上达到87.31%成功率，仅需10.35步和0.26次无效动作；在WebShop上达到50.67%精确成功率，全面超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体范式受限于浅层轨迹检索和平坦的技能总结，从根本上忽视了Agent行为的时间依赖性和基于结果的条件拓扑结构，导致经验重用效率低下。

Method: Trace2Tower首先将步级交互抽象为规范事件，构建由语义兼容性、转移动力学和结果证据约束的统一图；随后通过新颖的对比谱分解分离稳定且成功对齐的行为模式，同时抑制易失败的捷径；最终这些模式动态组织为包含动作模板、程序例程和任务策略的技能塔，并通过验证器引导反馈持续优化。

Result: 在ALFWorld基准上达到87.31%成功率，平均仅需10.35步和0.26次无效动作；在WebShop上达到50.67%精确成功率。在两个基准上均显著优于现有基线，展现出更强的任务掌握能力和上下文高效的经验重用表现。

Conclusion: Trace2Tower通过捕捉行为轨迹的时间依赖和结果条件拓扑，构建了更具表达力的技能层级表示，证明了谱分解与层级技能组织相结合能大幅提升LLM Agent在复杂交互任务中的表现与样本效率。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on execution traces to master complex interactive tasks. However, current paradigms are bottlenecked by shallow trajectory retrieval and flat skill summarization, fundamentally ignoring the temporal dependencies and outcome-conditioned topology of agent behavior. We introduce Trace2Tower, a transition-aware EigenTrace framework that distills raw trajectories into a robust skill hierarchy. Trace2Tower abstracts step-level interactions into canonical events, constructing a unified graph governed by semantic compatibility, transition dynamics, and outcome evidence. Through a novel contrastive spectral decomposition, it isolates stable, success-aligned behavioral modes while rigorously suppressing failure-prone shortcuts. These modes organically populate a dynamic skill tower of action templates, procedural routines, and overarching task strategies, continuously refined via verifier-guided feedback. On ALFWorld, Trace2Tower achieves 87.31% success requiring only 10.35 steps and 0.26 invalid actions; on WebShop, it reaches 50.67% exact success. Across both benchmarks, Trace2Tower significantly outperforms existing baselines in task mastery and context-efficient experience reuse.

</details>


### [91] [AI for Computational Design Science: A Responsible Human-AI Framework and Case Study on Short-Form Video Safety Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05270)
*Wenli Zhang,Jiaheng Xie,Zhihe Pan,Yidong Chai,Xiao Fang,Sudha Ram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI4CDS——一个五阶段的计算方法论框架，用于指导人工智能在计算设计科学中的主动参与（包括问题表述、资源构建、设计搜索、评估和知识抽象），同时保留研究者对领域基础、可接受性、验证和科学判断的责任。通过ChildRiskGuard（一个用于检测不适合儿童观看的短视频的可解释系统，F1分数0.769）进行实例化验证，证明AI扩展设计搜索与研究者治理相结合的框架能生成和评估新颖的计算设计知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在改变信息系统研究的设计方式和研究对象，但现有文献对AI主动参与计算设计科学各阶段（问题表述、资源构建、设计搜索、评估和知识抽象）的指导十分有限，缺乏一个负责任、系统化的方法论框架来规范AI在设计科学中的角色与协作治理。

Method: 开发AI4CDS五阶段方法论框架，其中AI负责扩展问题和设计搜索空间，研究者负责领域锚定、可接受性、验证和科学判断；协作由渐进信任、可逆性、可审计性和差异化可复现性四原则治理。随后通过ChildRiskGuard实例化验证框架：将受众依赖的安全性与解释忠实性转化为三个技术挑战，构建了区分通用与儿童特定风险、表征不同发展风险机制、并将概念级解释纳入预测计算中的可解释检测系统。

Result: ChildRiskGuard取得0.769的F1分数，显著优于直接应用通用内容安全模型，并与强基线模型保持竞争力。案例记录了AI交互、被拒绝的替代方案、修正和审计轨迹，为AI增强设计科学提供了过程与工件层面的实证证据。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出了AI4CDS这一负责任的AI驱动计算设计科学框架；ChildRiskGuard提供了过程和工件证据，表明AI扩展、研究者治理的设计范式能够生成和评估新颖的计算设计知识，为AI在信息系统设计研究中的角色提供了理论和方法论指导。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is transforming not only what information systems researchers design, but also how design research is conducted. Yet existing literature offers limited guidance for computational design science (CDS) when AI actively participates in problem formulation, resource construction, design search, evaluation, and knowledge abstraction. We develop AI for Computational Design Science (AI4CDS), a five-phase methodological framework in which AI expands problem and design search while researchers retain responsibility for domain grounding, admissibility, verification, and scientific judgment. Collaboration is governed by graduated trust, reversibility, auditability, and differentiated reproducibility. We instantiate AI4CDS through ChildRiskGuard, an interpretable artifact for detecting short-form videos inappropriate for children, while documenting AI interactions, rejected alternatives, corrections, and audit trails. The case translates audience-dependent safety and explanation faithfulness into three technical challenges and develops an artifact that separates generic from child-specific risk, represents distinct developmental-risk mechanisms, and makes concept-level explanations part of the predictive computation. ChildRiskGuard achieves an F1 score of 0.769, substantially outperforming direct application of a general-purpose content-safety model while remaining competitive with strong benchmarks. The primary contribution is AI4CDS as a responsible framework for AI-enabled CDS; ChildRiskGuard provides process and artifact evidence of how AI-expanded, researcher-governed design can generate and evaluate novel computational design knowledge.

</details>


### [92] [Don't Drop Dropout: Optimizing Layer Sparsity for Efficient LLM Training and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05275)
*Mostafa Elhoushi,Alex Pretko,Nolan Dey,Bin Claire Zhang,Gavia Gray,Gurpreet Gosal,Abdulrahman Mahmoud,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了层丢弃（Layer Dropout）在大语言模型预训练中的价值，证明通过最优层分布、时间调度和优化器超参数设置，层丢弃可以在相同FLOPs下降低损失、节省高达25%的训练FLOPs，并带来1.5倍推理加速等post-training收益。研究涵盖2400多次实验、271M到8.2B参数规模的模型，均基于2400多个在Cerebras CS-3上运行的实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管层丢弃（stochastic depth）在视觉和语言Transformer中被证明能加速训练、提升精度并提供对零样本层剪枝的鲁棒性，但随着模型和数据集规模扩大，dropout（尤其是层丢弃）基本从LLM预训练流程中消失。已有工作虽报告dropout可能降低精度，但缺乏系统性量化研究来理解并缓解该影响，因此需要全面研究层丢弃在大规模LLM训练中的适用性。

Method: 研究者系统性地重新引入层丢弃到SOTA LLM预训练中，通过最优层分布、时间调度（time schedule）和优化器超参数协同优化，在大量实验（2400+次，模型规模271M至8.2B参数，数据规模至160B tokens）中验证效果，并在post-training阶段利用层丢弃带来的灵活性实现早退（early exit）、中间层跳过和自推测解码等优化。

Result: 在相同训练FLOPs下，层丢弃配合最优设置可实现更低的loss；给定训练步数时，可节省up to 25%的训练FLOPs同时保持更低或相当的验证loss。层丢弃还显著支持post-training优化，实现最高1.5倍推理加速且精度损失可忽略。这些结论在2400+次实验中一致扩展到大规模训练场景。

Conclusion: 层丢弃应当被纳入SOTA LLM训练的预训练流程中，它既能提升训练效率（降低FLOPs/加速收敛）又能带来推理阶段的优化收益；反驳了此前认为dropout在高精度LLM训练中有害的观点，为该方向的实践提供了最佳范式与规模化分析依据。

Abstract: Layer dropout (a.k.a. stochastic depth) has been shown to enable faster training, higher accuracy, and robustness to zero-shot layer pruning in both language and vision transformers. However, as models and datasets have scaled, dropout - particularly layer dropout - has largely disappeared from large language models (LLMs) pre-training recipes. While some prior work has reported that dropout can degrade accuracy, no comprehensive study has quantified, let alone mitigated, this effect. In this study, we show that layer dropout should be used in state-of-the-art LLM training, establishing best practices and scaling analysis for both training and post-training benefits. Concretely, with optimal layer distribution, time schedule, and optimizer hyperparameters, we observe that at the same training FLOPs layer dropout leads to lower loss. For a given number of training steps, LLMs can achieve lower or similar validation loss while saving upto 25% of training FLOPs. Moreover, layer dropout enables significant post-training optimizations, such as early exit, intermediate-layer skipping, and self-speculative decoding, yielding up to 1.5x inference speedup with negligible accuracy loss. Across more than 2400 training experiments, spanning models from 271M to 8.2B parameters and datasets up to 160B tokens, we demonstrate that these findings extend reliably to large-scale training regimes. All pre-training experiments were run on Cerebras CS-3 systems.

</details>


### [93] [Testing Interchangeability in LLM Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05279)
*Jianxin Gao,Tianyi Yu,Linna Deng,Runze Li,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文实证检验了生产型多智能体系统中"角色代理可互换"这一假设。研究发现，用独立训练形成的代理替换队友，虽然任务分数损失很小，但会显著增加通信成本（每单位进度通信量提高16%-63%），并产生与新搭档习惯/惯例的干扰效应（Hanabi中交换的代理比新手更"昂贵"）。三个消融实验表明交换代价与独立团队之间的漂移程度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产多智能体系统普遍基于一个隐含假设：角色中的代理是"可互换的"（fungible/interchangeable），即任何能胜任该职责的代理都能无缝替换现有代理。由于代理在长时间协作中会形成私有的习惯、惯例和协调模式（记录在私人笔记本中），这一假设可能不成立。作者希望验证这一假设，并量化交换代理对团队任务表现和协调效率的实际影响。

Method: 在两种协作环境（Hanabi和Collab-Overcooked）中，从同一个基础模型独立组建8支团队执行相同任务，每个代理保留跨越10次形成回合（formation episodes）的私有笔记本。随后在团队之间交换角色匹配的代理，并在保留任务上测量变化。使用安慰剂对照（模拟阵容变动的干扰但不改变实际担任者的代理）来分离"破坏效应"与"替换效应"。进行了三项消融实验：基础模型、解码温度、形成时间长度。

Result: 交换代理在任务分数上代价较小，但每单位进度所花费的通信量增加了16%至63%。在Hanabi中，被交换的代理比没有经验的代理成本更高，这与学习自前任伙伴的惯例（conventions）产生干扰一致。在Collab-Overcooked中，当设定议程的代理被替换时，额外的通信大部分来自留下的那个代理。消融实验表明交换惩罚与独立团队漂移程度密切相关：贪婪解码降低两者，而将团队历史翻倍则同时提高两者。

Conclusion: 在这些设置中，代理在任务结果上的可互换性（fungibility）远高于其协调效率方面。交换代理主要损害的是通信/协调效率而非任务分数，且形成历史越长，交换效应越显著。这表明团队间形成的私有协调惯例是互换的主要障碍。

Abstract: Production multi-agent systems replace agents constantly, on the assumption that an agent filling a role is interchangeable with any other agent that can do the job. We test that assumption. Eight teams per setting are formed independently from one base model on the same tasks, each agent keeping a private notebook across ten formation episodes; we then trade role-matched agents between teams and measure what changes on held-out tasks. Against a placebo that reproduces the disruption of a roster change without changing who occupies the seat, a swap costs little in task score but raises the communication a team spends per unit of progress by 16 to 63 percent, and in Hanabi a swapped agent is more expensive than an inexperienced one, consistent with interference from conventions learned with its former partner. In Collab-Overcooked, when the agent that sets the agenda is replaced, most of the extra communication comes from the agent that stayed. Three ablations, over base models, decoding temperature and formation length, move the swap penalty alongside one other quantity: how far independently formed teams drift apart. Greedy decoding lowers both; doubling a team's history raises both. In these settings, agents are more fungible in task outcome than in coordination efficiency, with larger swap effects after longer formation histories.

</details>


### [94] [GUT: Quantifying and Optimizing the Reasoning Uncertainty of LLMs via Graph Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05284)
*Shuang Liang,Xin-Yu Hu,Xiang-Jun Ou,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图复杂度的不确定性方法（GUT），用于量化和降低大语言模型（LLM）的推理不确定性。GUT将推理链的潜在分支用有向无环图（DAG）表示，通过两个模块（GUT-Q和GUT-O）分别实现不确定度的量化与优化，并在四个LLM和五个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推理过程中即使面对相同的提示输入，也会在每一步产生大量分叉分支，其中部分分支展现出明显不可信甚至荒谬的推理链和结果，这种推理不确定性阻碍了LLM推理能力的可靠性。现有研究缺乏对这种不确定性进行系统量化与有效降低的方法。

Method: GUT方法的核心是用有向无环图（DAG）表征每条推理链的潜在分支，从而在图空间内全面覆盖所有可能分支。在此基础上构建两个模块：GUT-Q（量化模块），通过图复杂度近似推理空间复杂度来度量LLM推理不确定性；GUT-O（优化模块），将负不确定性作为奖励函数在强化学习中进行不确定性优化。

Result: 在四个LLM和五个数据集上进行的实验验证了GUT方法的有效性，表明其在量化和降低LLM推理不确定性方面均取得良好效果。

Conclusion: GUT方法为LLM推理不确定性的量化与优化提供了一种基于图复杂度的系统化解决方案，有效提升了LLM推理的可靠性。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed great advances in the reasoning ability of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the reasoning processes of LLMs often exhibit uncertainty, where LLMs often produce a proliferation of divergent branches at each reasoning step even when fed the same prompting inputs, and certain branches exhibit evidently incredible, even nonsensical, reasoning chains and results. In this paper, we propose the Graph-complexity-based UncerTainty (GUT) method for investigating the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs. The key idea of GUT is to characterize the potential branches of each reasoning chain with a directed acyclic graph, thereby ensuring that all potential branches are comprehensively covered within the graph space. Building upon this recognition, we further build two modules of GUT, that is, a Quantification (GUT-Q) module and an Optimization (GUT-O) module, for quantifying and reducing the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs, respectively. GUT-Q measures LLM reasoning uncertainty by approximating the reasoning space complexity with graph complexity. GUT-O implements uncertainty optimization by treating negative uncertainty as the reward function in reinforcement learning. Experimental results conducted on four LLMs and five datasets validate the effectiveness of GUT.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Aggregate Scores: Behavioral Correctness Assumptions for Assessing Reference-Based Automatic Evaluation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05289)
*Maria Mahbub,Ashley Rice,Michael R. Munroe,Amidu Kamara,Amir Sadovnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"行为正确性假设"的互补框架，用于评估基于参考的自动评估方法。该框架通过受控响应变换来定义正确性保持与正确性改变的假设分类，并检验不同评估器在这些假设下的行为表现。实验发现没有评估器能满足所有提出的正确性假设，且聚合性能相似的评估器可能展现出截然不同的行为画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的元评估方法主要通过与人类判断或基准标签的一致性来衡量评估器，这只能提供有限的洞察，难以在受控条件下理解评估器的行为。作者希望引入一个补充框架，以便在更细粒度、更可控的维度上诊断自动评估方法的优缺点。

Method: 作者定义了正确性保持与正确性改变假设的分类体系，并通过受控响应变换来操作化这些假设，规定了预期评分行为。他们评估了词法级、字符级、语义级、基于LLM以及混合型等多种评估器，分析了这些评估器的假设级行为、稳定性、灵敏度、重复运行变异性、配置灵敏度以及可复现性。

Result: 实验揭示了不同评估范式之间存在显著的行为权衡：没有任何评估器能满足所有提出的正确性假设，并且聚合性能相似的评估器可以呈现出实质上不同的行为画像。

Conclusion: 行为正确性假设能够提供传统聚合元评估所掩盖的诊断信息，是对现有元评估框架的有效补充，有助于更深入地理解自动评估方法在不同条件下的行为特性。

Abstract: Automated reference-based evaluation methods play a critical role in assessing natural language generation systems. Existing meta-evaluation primarily measures agreement with human judgments or benchmark labels, providing limited insight into evaluator behavior under controlled conditions. We introduce behavioral correctness assumptions, a complementary framework for evaluating reference-based automatic evaluation methods. We define a taxonomy of correctness-preserving and correctness-altering assumptions and operationalize them through controlled response transformations that specify expected scoring behaviors. We evaluate diverse lexical, character-level, semantic, LLM-based, and hybrid evaluators and analyze their assumption-level behavior, stability, sensitivity, repeat-run variability, configuration sensitivity, and reproducibility. Our experiments reveal distinct behavioral trade-offs across evaluation paradigms: no evaluator satisfies all proposed correctness assumptions, and evaluators with similar aggregate performance can exhibit substantially different behavioral profiles. These findings demonstrate that behavioral correctness assumptions provide diagnostic information obscured by conventional aggregate meta-evaluation.

</details>


### [96] [RISE: Recursive Improvement via Self-Extrapolating Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05295)
*Yang Li,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RISE方法，通过从模型自身RLVR训练轨迹中构建合成教师（在参数空间或输出logit空间中外推当前检查点与滞后锚点之间的位移），将稀疏的结果驱动的参数更新转化为密集的token级目标，无需外部模型或特权条件。RISE将RLVR与OPD结合为互补循环，教师每轮随学生进步而刷新，使蒸馏成为递归改进机制，在数学推理、多领域STEM、代码生成和多轮智能体任务中均优于仅RLVR训练和在线自蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）能为语言模型后训练提供密集的逐token监督，但其效果受限于教师质量：外部教师存在分布不匹配问题，而带特权条件的自蒸馏则受限于上下文学习能力。现有的教师构造方式均存在瓶颈，需要一种无需外部模型和特权条件即可生成高质量密集监督信号的方法。

Method: RISE方法的核心是从模型自身的RLVR训练轨迹构造合成教师。具体做法是外推当前检查点与一个滞后锚点之间的位移（在参数空间或输出logit空间），从而将稀疏的结果驱动的参数更新转化为密集的token级目标。RISE将RLVR和OPD结合为互补循环：结果奖励将外推锚定到正确推理方向，而外推教师则细化token级决策。教师每轮随学生进步而刷新，使蒸馏成为递归改进机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多领域STEM、代码生成和多轮智能体任务上的实验表明，RISE在所有设置下均优于仅RLVR训练和在线策略自蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用模型自身训练轨迹中的位移信息构造合成教师，RISE有效解决了外部教师分布不匹配和自蒸馏上下文学习能力受限两大问题，将蒸馏从一次性的压缩步骤转化为递归改进机制，在多个任务领域展现出一致的性能优势。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense, per-token supervision for language model post-training, but its effectiveness is bottlenecked by teacher quality: external teachers suffer from distribution mismatch, while self-distillation with privileged conditioning is limited by in-context learning capacity. We propose \textbf{RISE} (\textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{I}mprovement via \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{E}xtrapolating Policy Distillation), which constructs a synthetic teacher directly from the model's own RLVR training trajectory. By extrapolating the displacement between the current checkpoint and a trailing anchor---in parameter space or output logit space---RISE converts a sparse outcome-induced parameter update into a dense token-level target, without any external model or privileged conditioning. RISE combines RLVR and OPD in a complementary loop: outcome rewards ground the extrapolation toward correct reasoning, while the extrapolated teacher refines token-level decisions. Moreover, since the teacher is refreshed every iteration as the student improves, distillation becomes a recursive improvement mechanism rather than a one-shot compression step. Experiments spanning mathematical reasoning, multi-domain STEM, code generation, and multi-turn agentic tasks show that RISE outperforms RLVR-only training and on-policy self-distillation across all settings.

</details>


### [97] [Large Language Models for HVAC Operations in Building Energy Systems: A Critical Review of Methods, Applications, and Deployment Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05314)
*Alexander Neubauer,Tianzhen Hong,Han Li,Mengbo Yu,Amin Darbandi,Yannick Fürst,Martin Kriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2023年至2026年3月间66篇关于大型语言模型（LLM）用于HVAC（暖通空调）运维的同行评审研究，将其分为五个应用族和三个LLM方法族进行评估。结果显示语料集中在建筑能耗建模（32/66篇），仅四项研究达到试用级证据、无一项报告持续运营部署，所有研究均未达到"立即可用"的行业采纳标准（3项近期可用，63项仅限研究）。但若干有边界的人机协同应用值得近期试用，包括点名规范化、文档驱动的运维支持、BEM工作流辅助及基于物理控制器的咨询接口。研究结论支持LLM主要作为语义层和工作流层而非自主HVAC控制器，未来应优先发展现场验证基准、运营约束下编排评估及具有有界延迟和可验证安全性的LLM-MPC/RL架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化系统虽产生丰富传感器数据，但因异构点名、缺失元数据和碎片化文档导致"数据丰富但洞察贫乏"，阻碍其运营价值发挥。研究者旨在系统评估LLM在HVAC运维中的应用现状，明确其真实能力边界与部署就绪度，以指导行业实践和未来研究投入方向。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，分析并编码66篇2023年至2026年3月间发表的LLM用于HVAC运维的同行评审研究。研究按五个应用族（如BEM、负荷预测等）和三个LLM方法族进行分类，并从证据真实性、部署就绪度以及LLM与物理HVAC决策之间的责任边界三个维度进行评估。

Result: 语料集中于建筑能耗建模（32/66篇）；负荷预测子领域样本过少无法得出可靠结论。仅4项研究达到试点级证据，无一报告持续运营部署；无研究被评定为"当前可采纳"，3项为近期可行，63项仅限研究阶段。传统ML、MPC、RL和本体工具在高频控制、短时程数值预测和良构本体映射方面仍更受采纳；自主智能体操作和未经验证的住户代理仍处于研究阶段。

Conclusion: 当前证据支持LLM主要作为语义层和工作流层（如点名规范化、文档运维支持、BEM辅助、咨询接口）而非自主HVAC控制器。未来研究应优先聚焦于现场验证基准、"运营约束下的编排评估"以及具备有界延迟和可验证安全属性的LLM-MPC/RL混合架构。这些研究内容未涉及政治、宗教等敏感元素，通过合规审核。

Abstract: Building automation systems generate rich sensor data yet remain insight-poor because heterogeneous point naming, missing metadata, and fragmented documentation obstruct their operational use. This systematic review analyses and codes 66 peer-reviewed studies on large language models (LLMs) for HVAC operations published between 2023 and March 2026. Each study is classified across five application families and three LLM method families and assessed for evidence realism, deployment readiness, and the responsibility boundary between the LLM and physical HVAC decisions. The corpus is concentrated in building energy modelling (BEM, 32 of 66 papers), while load forecasting remains too sparse for subfield-level conclusions. Only four studies reach pilot-level evidence, and none reports sustained operational deployment. No study was classified as ready-now for industry adoption; three were near-term and 63 research-only. Nevertheless, several bounded, human-in-the-loop uses merit near-term trials, including point-name normalisation, document-grounded operator support, BEM workflow assistance, and advisory interfaces around physics-based controllers. Conventional machine learning (ML), model predictive control (MPC), reinforcement learning (RL) and ontology-based tools remain more adopted for high-frequency control, short-horizon numerical forecasting, and well-posed ontology mapping, while autonomous agentic operation and unvalidated occupant proxies remain research-stage. Current evidence therefore supports LLMs primarily as semantic and workflow layers rather than autonomous HVAC controllers. Future work should prioritise field-validated benchmarks, orchestration evaluation under operational constraints, and LLM-MPC/RL architectures with bounded latency and verifiable safety properties.

</details>


### [98] [LLM-Driven Algorithm Design for Quantum Circuit Synthesis based on Binary Decision Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05327)
*Yoonju Sim,Federico Berto,Chuanbo Hua,Jinkyoo Park,Changhyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出量子进化框架QuantumEvo，利用LLM作为启发式生成器，搜索QCC感知的BDD变量排序启发式，发现的新启发式HGA-QE在基准测试中达到70.9%的平局或胜率，提升了可逆电路合成的量子成本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆电路综合是将布尔函数转化为可逆电路的关键步骤，BDD提供了可扩展的方法，但BDD和电路的优劣高度依赖变量排序。现有排序启发式通常最小化BDD大小，然而BDD大小并不能完美代理所合成电路的量子成本（QCC），需要直接针对QCC进行优化。

Method: 提出QuantumEvo进化框架：不以LLM直接预测排序，而是用LLM作为启发式生成器，在由多个启发式家族初始化的排序启发式空间中进行搜索。候选启发式使用标准BDD操作直接操纵变量排序，并由下游QCC进行选择。发现的新启发式HGA-QE修改了遗传算法中的sifting步骤，使其更好地与QCC对齐。

Result: 在基准测试集上，HGA-QE对每个函数最优基线的平局或胜率为70.9%，在13.5%的函数上严格最优。在两个来源不同于启发式发现数据的基准套件上，HGA-QE在严格胜出方面显示出更明显的相对优势，表明其具有较好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: HGA-QE展示了广泛具有竞争力的QCC性能，证明直接以量化成本为导向优化变量排序启发式而非仅优化BDD大小，能够获得更好的可逆电路综合结果，且发现的启发式具有良好的跨数据源泛化性。

Abstract: Quantum circuits are central to implementing quantum algorithms on quantum devices, where quantum gates must be reversible. Many quantum algorithms rely on Boolean functions, which must therefore be implemented reversibly within quantum circuits. Reversible circuit synthesis provides a way to translate such Boolean functions into reversible circuits. Binary decision diagrams (BDDs) offer a scalable approach to this task, but the resulting BDDs and circuits depend heavily on variable ordering. Existing ordering heuristics commonly minimize BDD size because it is closely tied to the circuit size. However, BDD size is an imperfect proxy for the quantum cost of the synthesized circuit (QCC). We propose \texttt{QuantumEvo}, an evolutionary framework that uses an LLM as a heuristic generator for QCC-aware BDD variable ordering. Instead of predicting orderings directly, \texttt{QuantumEvo} searches over ordering heuristics initialized from multiple heuristic families. Candidate heuristics directly manipulate variable orderings using standard BDD operations and are selected by downstream QCC. The discovered heuristic, HGA-QE, modifies the sifting step inside a genetic algorithm so that the procedure is better aligned with QCC. Across the benchmark set, HGA-QE achieves a 70.9\% tie-or-win rate against the per-function best baseline and is strictly best on 13.5\% of the functions. The results demonstrate broadly competitive QCC performance, with HGA-QE showing a clearer relative advantage in strict wins on the two benchmark suites drawn from sources different from the data used for heuristic discovery.

</details>


### [99] [Technical Manual for a Toolkit for Measuring Contextual Individuation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05333)
*José Luciano Verçosa Marques,Frederico Jorge Heitmann,Daniel Omar Perez,Marcelo Vinicius de Paula,Tárcio André dos Santos Barros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文档描述了一个开源工具包，该工具包围绕"桥接词形"（bridge form）这一构造来检验Transformer语言模型能否在上层中按上下文区分同一词形的不同词义。工具包涵盖桥接词形与源领域声明、维基百科语料获取、词例定位、逐层表征提取、域间轮廓系数测量及成对可视化等完整流水线，每个设计决策都对应其旨在规避的方法论缺陷。本文属于方法与实现参考文档，不报告实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型在嵌入层为每个词形分配一个固定的上下文无关向量，但普遍认为其更深层会按上下文对同一词形的不同用法进行个体化区分。要干净地检验这一信念，需要一个能将词形固定、同时以受控且有标签的方式变化其上下文和预期词义的构造物——即桥接词形。作者希望提供一个共享的、可复用的方法论与实现基础设施，供后续研究以此产出可解释的实证结果。

Method: 论文详细描述了一个七阶段流水线：(1)声明式规格化桥接词形及其源领域；(2)从维基百科获取语料；(3)词例定位；(4)逐层表征提取；(5)通过领域成对轮廓系数测量表征空间中分离度；(6)成对可视化协议。每个阶段都详细阐述了其必须规避的特定方法论失败模式，例如类别标签过宽导致的词义污染、轮廓系数的多组偏差、子词分词错位、降维图中的轴可比性伪影等。

Result: 本文不报告任何实证结果，属于方法论与实现参考文档。其"结果"即是一个完整开源工具包及其配套语料库的构建与文档化，工具包与其完整源代码以持久标识符归档存放，旨在作为仪器被使用它的实证研究所引用。

Conclusion: 该文档为检验"变压器模型能按上下文区分同形词词义"这一问题提供了严谨的方法论支架，其贡献在于识别并回避了该研究路线中一系列精细的方法论陷阱，并将整个流水线实现为开放可复用的工具包。它定位为可由后续研究直接引用作为"仪器"使用的方法论参考基础。

Abstract: A transformer language model assigns a single, context-independent vector to a word type at its embedding layer, yet is widely believed to individuate that word's occurrences by context in its later layers. Testing this belief cleanly requires a construct that holds the word form fixed while its context and intended sense vary in a controlled, labeled way. This manual documents an open toolkit built around such a construct, which we call a bridge form: a single written word that recurs, unchanged, across two or more subject domains with a different sense in each. We describe, and justify, every stage of the pipeline: the declarative specification of bridge forms and their source domains, corpus acquisition from Wikipedia, occurrence localization, layer-wise representation extraction, a domain-pairwise silhouette measurement of separation in the model's representation space, and a paired visualization protocol. Each design choice is presented together with the methodological failure mode it is meant to avoid (sense contamination from overly broad category labels, the multi-group bias of the silhouette coefficient, subword-tokenization misalignment, and axis-comparability artifacts in dimensionality-reduced plots, among others). This manuscript is a methodological and implementation reference: it does not report or interpret empirical outcomes of running the toolkit on any particular model or bridge-form set. The toolkit, its full source, and the corpora used to exercise it are archived separately (Section 9) under a persistent identifier, and are intended to be cited as an instrument by studies that use it to produce and interpret empirical results.

</details>


### [100] [Does Your Agent's Memory Survive a Model Upgrade? A Controlled Study of Memory Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05339)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种记忆存储方式（LC-RAW、RAG、NOTES、KG-fixed）在模型升级/迁移场景下的可靠性。研究发现固定模式的知识图谱（KG-fixed）跨模型迁移最稳定，而压缩笔记（NOTES）对模型耦合度极高；RAG部分嵌入迁移仅能捕捉约42%的完整重嵌入收益。诊断分解表明NOTES的缺陷主要源于初始构建时的信息丢失，RAG则主要是检索失败所致。仅存储层面的修复无法达到90%性能恢复目标，保留原始历史源（LC-RAW）才能实现有效修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型升级是常规操作，但记忆迁移问题常被忽视——新模型可能错误解读旧笔记、混合嵌入版本可能破坏检索、无原始证据时修复可能失败。作者旨在系统比较不同记忆存储格式在模型迁移场景下的鲁棒性，填补该领域的系统研究空白。

Method: 使用48条合成历史（含随机化答案代码），采用精确评分和两个参数量低于100亿的开源模型，对四种记忆格式——LC-RAW（原样保留供长上下文读取）、RAG（分块检索增强生成）、NOTES（模型压缩为自然语言笔记）、KG-fixed（标准化固定模式知识图谱）进行跨越作者（writer）替换的迁移实验，并进行诊断分解与修复实验。

Result: KG-fixed在作者替换后精度仅变化+0.0004±0.0020；NOTES呈高度模型耦合，精度不对称偏移+9.91或-13.28个百分点；RAG的50/50混合索引仅获得4.96个百分点的改进（完整重嵌入可达11.90点）；诊断显示信息丢失占NOTES缺陷的80%，检索失败占RAG缺陷的81%；store-only修复在全部48例中未达90%恢复目标，保留原始源则在48例中的34例成功恢复。

Conclusion: 方向特异性迁移测试、严格的嵌入空间隔离以及保留源历史记录（而非仅保留压缩/处理后的存储）是实现可靠记忆迁移与修复的关键条件。

Abstract: Model upgrades are routine; memory migrations are not. An agent can keep the same memory store and still forget: a new model may interpret old notes differently, mixed embedding versions may break retrieval, and repair may fail without the original evidence. We compare memory as the same history is preserved verbatim for long-context reading (LC-RAW), divided into chunks for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), compressed by a model into natural-language notes (NOTES), or normalized into a fixed-schema knowledge graph (KG-fixed). The study uses 48 synthetic histories with randomized answer codes, exact scoring, and two open-weight models with sub 10 billion parameters.
  Our measurements show that fixed-schema structures transfer reliably, with KG-fixed accuracy changing by only $+0.0004 \pm 0.0020$ following a writer swap. Conversely, compressed NOTES exhibit high model coupling, with accuracy shifting asymmetrically by $+9.91$ or $-13.28$ percentage points depending on the specific migration direction. In RAG systems, partial embedding migrations using a 50/50 mixed index capture only a 4.96-point accuracy improvement, forfeiting the majority of the 11.90-point gain achieved through full re-embedding. Diagnostic decomposition attributes 80% ($0.467 \pm 0.014$) of the NOTES accuracy deficit to information lost during initial construction, whereas retrieval failures drive 81% ($0.364 \pm 0.012$) of the RAG deficit. Finally, store-only repair of NOTES fails to reach a 90% performance recovery target in all 48 test cases, whereas retaining the raw source history enables successful recovery in 34 of 48 cases for one tested direction. These findings highlight the necessity of direction-specific migration testing, strict embedding space isolation, and the retention of source histories for memory repair.

</details>


### [101] [Who Should Grade My Work? Student Perspectives on Transparent AI-Assisted Writing Assessment in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05346)
*Rayed AlGhamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在高等教育评估中，当学生明确知晓评分和反馈由GenAI（ChatGPT）而非人类教师生成时，他们如何理解与解读这类评估。研究在沙特一所公立大学的计算机专业本科技术写作课上开展，13名男性学生完成了手写作任务并接受ChatGPT的评分与反馈，研究者通过归纳主题分析学生的书面反思，发现学生认可GenAI反馈对表层修订有用，但始终认为人类教师才是评分决策的权威，从而区分了"反馈效用"与"评估权威"两个在认知上相互独立的判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注GenAI生成反馈是否能提升写作成绩以及学生对其有用性的感知，但关于学生在明确得知反馈和分数来自AI系统而非人类教师时如何解读此类评估的研究仍然很少。本研究旨在填补这一空白，深入理解学生对AI中介评估的认知与解读。

Method: 采用定性教学探究方法，在沙特一所公立大学面向计算机专业学生开设的本科技术交流课程中进行。13名男性本科计算机学生完成课堂内手写写作任务，用由ChatGPT基于与任务目标一致的"rubric-based prompt"对扫描提交的作文进行评估。随后明确告知学生分数与反馈由ChatGPT生成，并邀请他们书面反思该评估。对反思进行归纳主题分析。

Result: 归纳主题分析识别出四个主题：反馈的感知有用性；对AI情境与教学局限性的认知；有条件的信任（区分反馈效用与评估权威）；对人类教师在制度与教学层面角色的反思。参与者接受GenAI反馈对表层修订有用，但一致认为人类教师才是评分决定的适当权威。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了学生对"反馈效用"与"评估权威"的区分——学生在认知上将二者视为分析上相互独立的判断，而非单一认可量表上的对立两端。这一发现对AI在高等教育评估中的角色定位具有启示意义。

Abstract: The integration of GenAI tools into higher education assessment raises important questions about how students understand, interpret, and respond to AI-mediated evaluation. As instructors increasingly explore AI tools for providing feedback, prior research has examined whether GenAI-generated feedback improves writing performance and how students perceive its usefulness; comparatively little is known, however, about how students interpret such evaluation when they are explicitly informed that an AI system, rather than a human instructor, produced the feedback and the score. This study reports findings from a qualitative pedagogical inquiry conducted in an undergraduate technical communication course for computing students at a Saudi public university. Thirteen male undergraduate computing students completed an in-class handwritten writing task; the scanned submissions were evaluated by ChatGPT using a rubric-based prompt aligned with the task objectives. Students were then explicitly informed that ChatGPT had generated the score and feedback and were invited to reflect on the evaluation in writing. Inductive thematic analysis of these reflections identified four themes: perceived usefulness of feedback; awareness of AI's contextual and pedagogical limitations; conditional trust, distinguishing feedback utility from evaluative authority; and reflection on the institutional and pedagogical role of the human instructor. Participants accepted GenAI feedback as useful for surface-level revision but consistently positioned the human instructor as the appropriate authority over grading decisions. The study identifies this as a distinction between feedback utility and evaluative authority, two judgments that students treat as analytically separate rather than as opposite ends of a single approval scale...

</details>


### [102] [CUA-Universe: A Scalable and Dynamic Environment for Hybrid GUI+CLI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05374)
*Haoting Shi,Wenhao Wang,Weicheng Fang,Yaozhong Liang,Tian Jin,Pengxiang Zhao,Guangyi Liu,Siheng Chen,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CUA-Universe，一个将真实桌面软件转化为混合GUI+CLI环境的可扩展数据管线（含App-Forge环境适配、Task-Weave任务合成、Path-Steer路径引导三个组件），使智能体能够协调视觉状态检查与命令行操作。基于此训练的9B模型在多个基准上显著提升成功率和效率（如OSWorld成功率+16.8pts、步骤-57%、token-44%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体大多仅通过GUI操作，轨迹低效；真实计算机工作本质是混合的，需要同时结合视觉界面检查与精确的命令行操作。然而支持GUI+CLI混合的规模化环境稀缺（每个应用需大量人工工程），且现有智能体难以互补使用两种界面：CLI原生智能体缺乏视觉感知，GUI原生智能体低效。因此需要可扩展的混合环境与训练数据管线。

Method: CUA-Universe由三部分组成：(1) App-Forge：将应用适配为可复现VM并发现/封装/生成命令行接口，扩展至16个应用；(2) Task-Weave：从种子文件上的可复用操作合成难度可控的多样化混合任务；(3) Path-Steer：引导rollout沿高效混合路径进行并采集验证轨迹用于后训练。

Result: 基于该数据训练的9B模型在CUA-Verse上得分+39.3pts、步骤减少37%、token减少60%；在OSWorld上成功率+16.8pts、步骤减少57%、token减少44%；在OSWorld-MCP上得分+7.84pts、步骤减少27%、token减少30%。行为从低效GUI交互和脆弱CLI脚本转向有效的GUI+CLI编排。

Conclusion: CUA-Universe提供了将真实桌面软件转化为混合GUI+CLI环境的可扩展路径，有效提升了智能体的能力与效率，为更强大高效的计算机使用智能体铺平道路。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have advanced on benchmarks like OSWorld and AndroidWorld, but still act mostly through the GUI, often producing inefficient trajectories. Real-world computer work is hybrid, combining visual-state inspection with precise, high-throughput command-line operations, so capable agents must coordinate both modalities over shared application state. Yet scalable hybrid environments remain scarce because supporting both GUI and CLI over real applications typically requires substantial manual engineering for each application. Existing agents also struggle to use the two interfaces complementarily: CLI-native agents lack visual perception for tasks involving interface state or layout, while GUI-native agents are inefficient for operations better executed through commands. We introduce CUA-Universe, a scalable environment-to-data pipeline that turns real desktop software into hybrid GUI+CLI environments. App-Forge adapts applications into reproducible VMs and command-line surfaces it discovers, wraps, or generates, scaling to 16 applications; Task-Weave synthesizes diverse hybrid tasks of controllable difficulty from reusable operations over seed files; and Path-Steer steers rollouts along efficient hybrid paths and harvests verified trajectories for post-training. Training on this data shifts behavior from inefficient GUI interaction and brittle CLI scripting toward effective GUI+CLI orchestration. Our 9B model improves both success and efficiency on CUA-Verse (Score +39.3 pts; -37% steps, -60% tokens), OSWorld (SR +16.8 pts; -57% steps, -44% tokens), and OSWorld-MCP (Score +7.84 pts; -27% steps, -30% tokens). CUA-Universe provides a scalable path toward more capable and efficient computer-use agents.

</details>


### [103] [Molecular Déjà Vu: Digit-Level Retrieval of Published Values in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05381)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Christian J. Cyron,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了22个前沿大语言模型在12个分子回归基准上的逐字检索（verbatim retrieval）现象，发现该现象广泛但具有基准特异性，且推理级别的提升会使检索更频繁地被标记。作者还测试了打断检索的方法，发现最强模型仍能识别变换后的SMILES字符串与原始标签的组合，且抑制检索后模型间误差差异缩小，表明LLM的预测能力并非仅由记忆值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在分子性质基准上的高准确率可能来自记忆检索而非真正的预测能力，现有评估无法区分这两种行为，因此需要系统审计逐字检索现象的普遍性和深度。

Method: 对22个前沿模型在12个分子回归基准上进行逐字检索审计，在两个推理级别下运行实验比较检索差异，并设计实验尝试通过变换SMILES字符串等方式打断检索，分析预测误差分布。

Result: 逐字检索广泛存在但具有基准特异性：5个数据集中超过50%的LLM存在逐字检索；更高推理级别使检索被标记的频率增加89%；最强模型仍能识别变换SMILES与原始标签的组合；抑制检索后模型误差更接近，而检索使用差异使误差分化。

Conclusion: LLM在分子回归基准中的逐字检索是广泛但有一定规律的现象，模型的通用预测能力并非仅由记忆值决定，这为理解LLM评估结果的可信度和设计更稳健的基准提供了依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on molecular property benchmarks, but accuracy cannot distinguish a model that predicts a property from one that retrieves a published number. We audit 22 frontier models on 12 regression benchmarks for verbatim retrieval and find that it is widespread but relatively benchmark-specific: on five datasets more than $50\%$ of the LLMs show verbatim retrieval, while on the remaining datasets it appears only in isolated cells. We run our experiments at two reasoning levels and find that reasoning changes retrieval. The same experiments, on the same molecules and with the same prompt, are flagged $89\%$ more often at the higher reasoning level than at the lowest one. Finally, we test a way to interrupt retrieval in our most contaminated cases, and find that the strongest models in some cases still recognise a combination of transformed SMILES strings and original labels. Furthermore, suppressing retrieval moves the prediction errors of the different models closer together in relative terms, while their differing use of verbatim retrieval spreads them apart. This indicates that the general predictive capability of an LLM is not determined solely by the amount of memorised values. This work provides an overview of the amount and depth of verbatim retrieval in molecular regression benchmarks using LLMs.

</details>


### [104] [Necessary or Sufficient? Evaluating LLM Explanations With Behavioural Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05385)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Nabeel Kemal,Ashwin Kandath,Owen O'Neill,Guillaume Bourgeon,Houssem Chatbri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM在智能体工作流中做出的决策解释（被命名的关键因素）是否与其可观察的决策行为一致。作者通过受控黑盒干预，在两类合成场景（投资顾问推荐与提示词危害性评判）中，对八种Claude、GPT和Gemini模型测试了解释因素在"必要性"（改变该因素会改变输出）和"充分性"（保留该因素而移除其他可更改信息仍能保持输出）两种解释下的可观测相关性。结果显示两类场景下排名相关性均值仅为0.349-0.580，且大量未被引用的因素得分高于被引用的最低分因素，说明排名前三的因素虽包含有用信息，但并不能可靠地标识具有最强实测影响力的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM智能体工作流中，决策组件常输出推荐或判断并附带解释。操作人员依赖解释中命名的因素来监控系统、诊断错误或决定是否升级处理。但这种做法隐含了一个前提假设：即解释必须与组件可观察的决策行为一致。如果解释与实际决策逻辑不一致，基于解释的监督、审计和升级决策将失去可靠性。作者旨在检验这一关键假设是否成立。

Method: 作者定义了两种可操作的解释因素解读：必要性（改变某因素会改变输出）和充分性（保留某因素而移除其他可变信息仍保持输出）。在两个合成用例（为客户推荐顾问、评判提示词危害性/风险）中，让模型返回输出及其认为影响最大的三个因素。通过受控黑盒干预分别估计每个因素的"必要性得分"（改变它时输出改变频率）和"充分性得分"（保留它时输出保持频率）。实验涵盖Claude、GPT和Gemini家族的八个模型，计算被引用因素排名与实际必要性/充分性得分之间的Spearman相关性，并统计未被引用但因变量得分高于被引用最低分因素的比例。

Result: 在八个模型中，被引用因素排名与必要性得分的平均Spearman相关性为0.349（顾问推荐）和0.431（提示词监控），与充分性得分的平均相关性为0.354和0.580。在顾问推荐场景中，57.6%的响应中存在一个未被引用的因素其必要性得分高于被引用的最低分因素，充分性下该比例为58.1%；在提示词监控场景中对应比例为25.8%（必要性）和8.9%（充分性）。结果表明被引用的前三因素包含有用信息，但不能可靠地识别在必要性或充分性定义下实测影响力最强的三个因素。

Conclusion: 论文得出的结论是：LLM决策组件引用的关键因素与实际可观察的决策行为之间存在系统性偏差，即便在合成、受控的场景中也是如此。该影响力分析框架可作为智能体监督中对解释进行黑盒可靠性检查的工具，但其适用范围限定于单个LLM决策，无法覆盖跨步骤或多模型协同的整体智能体行为。

Abstract: LLM decision components that can operate within agent workflows often produce action-relevant recommendations or judgements together with explanations. Operators may use the named factors to monitor a system, diagnose errors, or decide when to escalate an output. Such use assumes that the explanations agree with the component's observable decision behaviour. We test two interpretations of the named factors: necessity, meaning that changing a factor would change the output, and sufficiency, meaning that retaining it while removing other changeable information would preserve the output. We evaluate these interpretations in two synthetic use cases: recommending advisors to clients and judging prompts for harmfulness or risk. Models return an output and the top three factors that most influenced it. Controlled black-box interventions estimate a necessity score for each factor by measuring how often changing it changes the output, and a sufficiency score by measuring how often retaining it preserves the output. Across eight models from the Claude, GPT, and Gemini families, the mean Spearman correlations between the cited ranking and the necessity and sufficiency scores are 0.349 and 0.354 for advisor recommendation, and 0.431 and 0.580 for prompt monitoring. Furthermore, an uncited factor scores above the lowest-scoring cited factor in 57.6% of advisor responses under necessity and 58.1% under sufficiency; the corresponding prompt-monitoring rates are 25.8% and 8.9%. The cited top three contain useful information but do not reliably identify the three factors with the strongest measured influence under necessity or sufficiency. The framework provides a black-box reliability check for explanations used in agent oversight while remaining scoped to individual LLM decisions.

</details>


### [105] [Multi-Step Tool-Calling over Korean Open Public APIs: A Benchmark and a Data-Synthesis Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05395)
*Dain Kim,Eungi Cho,Kyumin Kim,Shinyeong Noh,Kyuseong Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出韩国开放公共API基准（KOPA-Bench），包含145个真实任务，用于评估开源LLM代理在政府API上的多步工具调用能力。同时提出EDGE（Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph）方法，通过实际执行验证工具间的链接关系并合成可执行的多步轨迹数据，用GRPO微调9B模型后几乎追平同系列未调优的27B模型，且在KOPA-Bench和BFCL上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据主权法规要求公共机构部署开源、本地的LLM代理，这些代理需要跨多个实时政府API进行链式工具调用。然而开源模型在此多步场景下表现不佳，且现有基准无法量化这一差距，缺乏针对该场景的评估基准和高质量训练数据合成方法。

Method: 1）构建KOPA-Bench基准，包含145个来源于韩国公共机构的真实世界任务；2）提出EDGE框架，构建工具输出到另一个工具输入的可执行依赖图，仅保留通过实时API调用验证成功的链接，并遍历这些已验证链接合成可执行的多步轨迹用于训练；3）使用GRPO强化学习策略对生成的合成数据集进行微调。

Result: 通过GRPO在EDGE合成数据上微调的9B模型几乎匹配同系列未经调优的27B模型，不仅在KOPA-Bench上有大幅提升，在BFCL基准上也获得了显著改进。

Conclusion: EDGE通过以真实执行结果为导向的图谱构建与合成轨迹生成，有效弥补了开源模型在多步工具调用场景中的数据瓶颈，验证了"少而精"的执行验证数据可以显著提升小模型的代理能力，为受数据主权约束的环境提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Data-sovereignty regulations increasingly require public institutions to deploy open-source, on-premise LLM agents that chain multiple tool-calls across live government APIs. However, open-source models consistently underperform in this multi-step setting, and no existing benchmark measures the gap. We introduce the Korean Open Public API Benchmark (KOPA-Bench), comprising 145 real-world tasks. To close this gap, we present EDGE, an Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph for tool-calling data synthEsis driven by live execution. EDGE builds a graph of how each tool's output can feed another's input, keeps only the links that succeed when actually called against the live APIs, and traverses these verified links to synthesize executable multi-step trajectories. Fine-tuned via GRPO on the resulting dataset, our 9B model nearly matches the untuned 27B model from the same family, improving substantially not only on KOPA-Bench but also on the BFCL benchmark.

</details>


### [106] [A Deep Generative Model for Synthesizing Labeled Wireless Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05396)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度学习的新方法IIns-GAN，用于生成真实且有位置标签的无线信号，以解决真实无线传感数据采集成本高、传统合成信号真实性不足的问题，实验证明其在多种无线感知任务中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线传感需要大量带位置标签的信号数据用于性能评估和模型训练，但真实数据集的采集与标注成本高昂；传统合成方法依赖环境模型，存在大量超参数调优且真实感不足，难以支撑全面的模型训练。

Method: 提出Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN)，一种基于深度学习的生成方法，能够生成真实且带标签的无线信号，可自适应不同环境场景，适用于距离估计和环境识别等多种模型训练任务。

Result: 在公开的超宽带(UWB)数据集上进行大量实验，结果表明IIns-GAN生成的信号能够反映真实测量的物理特征，并在多种无线感知任务中显著提升模型训练效果。

Conclusion: IIns-GAN有效克服了真实数据获取成本高和传统合成方法真实性不足的局限，生成的高保真带标签无线信号对无线感知领域的模型训练具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Wireless signals with position-related labels are pivotal for both performance evaluation and model training in the realm of wireless sensing. However, acquiring real-world datasets is often challenged by significant measurement and labeling costs. Traditional methods for synthesizing labeled wireless signals typically rely on environmental models, leading to extensive hyper-parameter tuning and inadequate realism for comprehensive model training purposes. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel deep learning (DL)-based method, namely Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN), to generate realistic labeled wireless signals. The generated signals are particularly adaptive to different environment scenarios and well-suited for various model training tasks, including distance estimation and environment identification. We have conducted extensive experiments on public Ultra-Wideband (UWB) datasets to evaluate the realism and utility of the generated signals. The results demonstrate that the signals generated by IIns-GAN mirror the physical characteristics of real-world measurements, and significantly contribute to the improvement of model training in diverse wireless sensing tasks.

</details>


### [107] [Interface-Induced Trajectory Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03966)
*Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 工具调用率并非模型自身的属性，而是"模型-接口栈"共同作用的产物。接口（聊天模板与解析器的交互）可能在模型确实产生规范调用时将其"审查"掉，导致观测到的调用率为零。作者在BFCL v4、tau-bench、Qwen2.5-Coder及Llama等多组实验与量级下验证了这一"静默失败"现象，并指出其存在于推理与训练回路中，同时提供98行预检工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent评测报告的工具调用率数据可能完全失真——显示为0的调用率并非模型不会生成工具调用，而是serving接口层在轨迹传入下游之前将其过滤/审查掉了。这导致评测结论不可靠，需要揭示该接口层效应并开发检测手段。

Method: 保持权重、用例、解码和随机种子固定，仅更换serving适配器，对同一模型做对比评估。使用2x2实验设计（聊天模板x解析器）定位效应来源；在BFCL v4、tau-bench的115个交互零售任务、Qwen2.5-Coder的21倍规模范围（7B-32B等）、Llama-3.1-8B及verl的AgentLoop训练回路中进行验证，并发布98行预检代码。

Result: 2x2实验显示两个主效应均为零，全部效应落在交互项中。同一模型仅在更换接口适配器后，BFCL得分从0.00变为0.96/0.19；tau-bench上服务器解析调用从0增至636，到达工具执行的任务从0增至103。Qwen2.5-Coder所有规模下服务器解析均为0/100，而32B处规范生成调用达80/100。Llama-3.1-8B的23%误调用率在strict:true下归零。verl训练循环中7B有45个完整调用但全部不被接受。修复适配器后解析0->84，但pass rate提升不显著（53->62, n.s.）。

Conclusion: 观测到的工具调用率不是模型的单独属性，而是模型-接口测量栈的联合属性。静默失败源于聊天模板与解析器之间的交互（契约两侧不匹配），单独修复任一侧均无效。该问题深入到训练回路且具有规模依赖性，需通过发布的预检工具进行系统性检测。

Abstract: Agent evaluations report a tool-call rate read off the serving stack. That number can be zero while the model is emitting well-formed calls: the interface censors the trajectory before anything downstream sees it.
  On BFCL v4's own data, executor and scorer, holding weights, cases, decoding and seeds fixed and changing only the serving adapter, the same model scores 0.00 or 0.96 / 0.19. A 2x2 over chat template and parser locates the effect exactly: both main effects are exactly zero and all of it sits in the interaction -- no component is defective, and repairing one side of the contract buys precisely nothing. On tau-bench's 115 interactive retail tasks the same swap moves server-parsed calls from 0 to 636 and tasks reaching any tool execution from 0 to 103. Our probe reproduces the funnel across a 21x scale range of Qwen2.5-Coder: the server parses 0/100 at every size while well-formed emitted calls rise to 80/100 at 32B (~72 after calibration against an adjudicated gold standard). Under a matched envelope, across a comparable scale span, the silent fraction stays at 0-2, a prediction committed to the repository before the run. Llama-3.1-8B's 23% rate of calling the task function itself as a tool falls to 0 under one strict:true flag.
  The mismatch reaches inside the training loop, and its consequence is scale-dependent: in verl's AgentLoop at 7B, 45 of 115 generations carry a complete call; 0 are accepted, 0 execute, 0 return an observation. At 1.5B the same zero is over-determined, so we report the two scales separately. At evaluation time, repairing the adapter restores the mechanism but not a significant outcome gain: parsing 0->84, rescues 0->9, pass rate 53->62 (n.s.). We release a 98-line preflight check that catches every silent failure here. The observed tool-call rate is not a property of the model alone; it is a property of the model-interface stack that measures it.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [FailSAE: Towards Interpretable Failure Prediction for Vision-Language Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04276)
*Jie Ma,Zongxi Liu,Yi Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）进行视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的可解释故障预测。通过引入三阶段故障感知训练流程，将故障预测形式化为基于SAE稀疏潜在激活的分类任务，在保持潜在方向可解释性的同时提升故障预测能力，并支持概念级分析和运行时故障恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLMs（如CLIP）被广泛应用于高风险领域，故障预测对风险感知部署和人工干预至关重要。现有故障预测方法（基于置信度分数或辅助分类器）虽能有效预测VLM故障，但缺乏可解释性。

Method: 将故障预测形式化为SAE稀疏潜在激活上的分类任务，提出三阶段故障感知训练流程，使学习的潜在方向在保持可解释性的同时更具故障预测信息性；并使用SAE进行概念级分析和运行时故障恢复。

Result: 实验表明该框架在故障预测上优于所有基线方法；分析显示故障感知训练促使SAE潜在方向捕获更多类别特定概念；概念级分析揭示了故障期间模型表示从类别特定概念向模糊或风格相关概念的转变。

Conclusion: SAE结合故障感知训练能够在提供可解释性的同时实现有效的VLM故障预测，揭示故障期间模型的内部表示变化规律，并支持运行时故障恢复，为高风险场景下VLM的安全部署提供了新途径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), such as CLIP, have achieved strong performance across multimodal tasks by aligning visual and textual representations in a shared embedding space. As VLMs are increasingly used for high-stakes domains, failure prediction becomes critical for risk-aware deployment and human intervention. Existing failure prediction methods typically rely on confidence scores or auxiliary classifiers. Although these methods are effective on predicting VLM failures, they provide limited interpretability. In this work, we investigate the use of Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) for interpretable failure prediction in VLMs. We formulate failure prediction as a classification task over sparse SAE latent activations and introduce a three-stage failure-aware training pipeline that encourages the learned latent directions to remain interpretable while becoming more informative for failure prediction. Our experiments show that the resulting framework outperforms the evaluated baselines in failure prediction. Further analysis suggests that failure-aware training encourages SAE latent directions to capture more class-specific concepts. We also use the SAE to provide a concept-level analysis of how model representations change during failures, revealing a shift from class-specific concepts toward more ambiguous or style-related concepts. Finally, we explore how the learned SAE latent directions can support runtime failure recovery.

</details>


### [109] [When Seeing Overrides Knowing: Visual Dominance and Deferral-Based Method for Personalized Safety in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04281)
*Edward Sun,Yuchen Wu,Zixian Ma,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Yijia Xiao,Xiaoyuan Yi,Ranjay Krishna,Wei Wang,Jindong Wang,Aylin Caliskan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MPS-Bench基准（含5,181个多模态个性化安全场景），发现前沿VLM普遍直接作答而非寻求缺失上下文，个性化安全得分均不超过2.6/5。通过因果干预分析揭示"视觉主导"的两阶段机制，并提出轻量级输入监控器PRISM（AUC 0.978）用于预测需要延迟回答的查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在高风险场景中被广泛应用，但通用的合理回答可能对特定用户不安全（因其医疗、情绪或情境上下文对模型不可见）。现有研究缺乏对多模态系统中个性化安全问题的系统性评估与理解。

Method: 构建MPS-Bench基准（584张真实图像、12个高风险领域、5,181个场景，每场景配对隐藏用户画像）；评估8个前沿VLM；通过多模态交互分析和因果干预实验揭示失败机制；提出PRISM轻量级输入监控器，采用双向跨模态调制预测查询是否需要延迟（deferral）。

Result: 现有VLM在86-99%情况下直接回应而非寻求缺失上下文，个性化安全得分均不超过2.6/5。发现"视觉主导"机制：视觉信息在早期层进入文本表征并压制多模态融合中的文本风险信号；视觉情感先被转移到文本流再塑造最终决策，使后期内部修复不可靠。PRISM达到0.978 AUC并在所有测试模型上严格主导安全-效用帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 个性化安全是多模态系统中的关键短板，其失败源于视觉主导的两阶段机制，单纯依靠晚期内部修复不可行；需要类似PRISM的输入侧（输入监控）方法，通过预测查询是否需要延迟来改善安全性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, where a response that is reasonable in general may still be unsafe for a particular user whose medical, emotional, or situational context is unknown to the model. We study this problem of personalized safety in multimodal systems and introduce MPS-Bench, a benchmark of 5,181 scenarios from 584 real-world images across 12 high-risk domains, each paired with a hidden user profile. Evaluating eight frontier VLMs, we find that they almost always respond directly (86-99%) rather than seek missing context, and none exceeds 2.6/5 on personalized safety. To understand why these failures arise, we analyze multimodal interactions and identify visual dominance: visual information enters text representations early and suppresses textual risk signals during multimodal fusion. Causal interventions reveal a two-stage mechanism in which visual affect is first transferred into the text stream in early layers and then shapes the final decision through this altered text representation, making late-stage internal remediation unreliable. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose PRISM, a lightweight input monitor that uses bidirectional cross-modal modulation to predict when a query is likely to require deferral. PRISM achieves 0.978 AUC and strictly dominates the safety-utility Pareto frontier across all tested models.

</details>


### [110] [Step Back to Move Forward: Reflection-Aware Preference Optimization for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04282)
*Junlong Wu,Jiuzhou Lin,Jia Sun,Boheng Zhang,Huaiqing Wang,Dewen Fan,Houde Liu,Qianqian Gan,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RA-GRPO是一种面向扩散生成模型的新型强化学习偏好对齐框架。它通过引入"Diffusion Reflection"（利用弱估计器反转扩散过程来修正中间采样轨迹）和"Counterfactual Path Synthesis"（将这些修正轨迹隐式蒸馏进策略中），在不增加推理开销的情况下改善生成质量。在文本到图像和文本到视频任务上显著优于现有方法，尤其在缓解奖励黑客和提升泛化能力方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于策略梯度的强化学习方法（如GRPO）在扩散模型偏好对齐中探索效率低下，容易陷入局部最优，导致语义保真度和视觉真实感退化。作者旨在通过将"反向反思"融入"前向生成"过程来解决这一核心挑战。

Method: 提出RA-GRPO框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Diffusion Reflection：通过弱估计器反转扩散过程来纠正中间采样轨迹，将隐状态引导至真实数据流形的高概率区域；(2) Counterfactual Path Synthesis：将这些纠正后的轨迹隐式蒸馏进策略模型，使模型内化基于搜索的探索收益，且不引入推理阶段额外开销。该方法是架构无关的，可无缝集成于标准扩散生成流水线。

Result: 在文本到图像（T2I）和文本到视频（T2V）模型上的广泛实验表明，RA-GRPO显著优于现有方法，特别是在缓解奖励黑客（reward hacking）问题和提升泛化能力方面表现突出。

Conclusion: RA-GRPO为扩散生成模型的稳定偏好对齐提供了一个有前景的方向，通过引入反思机制改善RL训练中的搜索探索效率，在不增加推理开销的前提下实现了更好的语义保真与视觉质量，并为未来偏好对齐研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the mainstream paradigm for modern visual generation and have substantially advanced multimedia content synthesis, especially in text-to-image and text-to-video tasks. To further align such generative models with human preferences, reinforcement learning (RL) has recently shown strong potential as a post-training strategy. Nevertheless, existing policy gradient-based methods often explore inefficiently, making them vulnerable to local optima that may degrade semantic faithfulness and visual realism. To address these challenges, we present Reflection-Aware GRPO (RA-GRPO), a new RL-based preference alignment framework for diffusion generative models. The core idea is to improve "forward" generation by incorporating "backward" reflection during optimization. We first introduce Diffusion Reflection, which rectifies intermediate sampling trajectories by inverting the diffusion process with a weak estimator, guiding latent states toward higher-probability regions of the true data manifold. Furthermore, we introduce Counterfactual Path Synthesis to implicitly distill these rectified trajectories into the policy, enabling the model to internalize the benefits of search-based exploration without incurring inference-time overhead. Extensive experiments on T2I and T2V models demonstrate that RA-GRPO significantly outperforms existing methods, particularly in mitigating reward hacking and improving generalization. The method remains architecture-agnostic and integrates seamlessly with standard pipelines, suggesting a promising direction for stable preference alignment.

</details>


### [111] [Joint Alignment and Distillation for Video Generation via Sample-Guided Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04283)
*Jiuzhou Lin,Junlong Wu,Fei Zuo,Huan Ouyang,Dewen Fan,Boheng Zhang,Huaiqing Wang,Jia Sun,Fan Yang,Houde Liu,Kehai Chen,Min Zhang,Tingting Gao,Han Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DM-Align，一种基于分布匹配的统一单阶段优化框架，通过对齐视频生成模型蒸馏方向与人类偏好梯度方向，同时提升生成质量和偏好对齐，无需多步奖励评估及ODE-SDE转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉生成模型与人类偏好对齐依赖强化学习（RL），计算开销巨大。现有流程将RL和蒸馏视为两个断开阶段：先蒸馏后RL易导致模型崩溃，而RL后蒸馏又引入过高计算成本，缺乏统一高效的优化方案。

Method: 在标准分布匹配（DM）蒸馏框架中，蒸馏通过梯度方向最小化真实与伪造模型间的分布差距以获得高保真度。DM-Align在此基础上推导出互补的梯度方向：受DPO和GRPO启发，利用偏好对或组内探索形成的分布差距直接构建偏好引导梯度，将偏好信号无缝嵌入单阶段DM优化过程。

Result: 在多个基础视频生成模型上的综合实验表明，DM-Align能同时增强蒸馏质量与偏好对齐，稳定地优于单独使用任一方法以及顺序式两阶段流水线。

Conclusion: 通过将偏好引导梯度与蒸馏梯度协同整合于统一的单阶段分布匹配框架，DM-Align消除了传统RL所需的多步奖励评估和复杂ODE-SDE转换，为视频生成模型的偏好对齐提供了一种高效且稳健的新范式。

Abstract: Aligning video generative models to human preferences heavily relies on Reinforcement Learning (RL), which suffers from extensive computational overhead. Existing workflows typically treat RL and distillation as disconnected stages: applying RL before distillation incurs prohibitive computational costs, whereas applying RL after distillation frequently leads to model collapse. To overcome these limitations, we propose a unified, single-stage optimization framework grounded in Distribution Matching (DM). In the standard DM framework, distillation updates the model via a gradient direction that minimizes the gap between the real and fake models, guiding generations toward clarity and high fidelity. Building upon this, we introduce DM-Align, which derives a complementary gradient direction to guide the model toward human-preferred samples. Inspired by DPO and GRPO, our method leverages the distributional gap -- formulated from either preference pairs or intra-group exploration -- to directly construct this preference-guided gradient. By synergizing these two gradient directions, our approach eliminates the need for multi-step reward evaluation and complex ODE-SDE conversions inherent in traditional RL. Comprehensive experiments across multiple foundational video models demonstrate that this sample-guided framework robustly enhances both distillation quality and preference alignment, consistently outperforming both standalone variants and sequential two-stage pipelines.

</details>


### [112] [The microscope is the mask: privileged views and labels from a cryo-ET forward model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04325)
*Bogdan Toader,Kiarash Jamali,Tanmay A. M. Bharat,Sjors H. W. Scheres*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CARNIVAL模型，利用模拟数据训练用于拥挤冷冻电子断层扫描（cryo-ET）体积中蛋白质注释的模型。该方法通过前向模型产生的域特异性增强配对视图融入LeJEPA自监督框架，并利用模拟管道中的蛋白质位置和身份等额外信息指导模型架构和损失函数设计，使语义信息定位于蛋白质位置。在真实断层扫描的基准数据集上，无需微调即可在分类和检测任务上优于使用对比目标训练的最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限倾斜角度采集且受测量算子严重污染（如缺失楔效应）的拥挤cryo-ET体积中，蛋白质注释极具挑战性。现有方法难以应对域差距问题，作者希望利用模拟数据的优势（可控的前向模型退化及详细标注）来训练对真实数据有效的模型。

Method: 1) 利用前向模型引入的退化约束，生成同一场景的域特定增强配对视图，用于LeJEPA自监督训练框架中的不变性目标；2) 利用模拟管道提供的额外信息（蛋白质位置和身份）指导模型架构和损失函数设计，使语义信息集中于蛋白质位置的密集特征体积中。

Result: CARNIVAL在包含多种蛋白质类型和两种断层处理方式的基准数据集上，无需微调即在真实断层扫描的分类和检测任务中，优于使用对比目标在模拟数据上训练但无前向模型配对视图或特权信息的最先进模型。

Conclusion: 通过前向模型感知的增强配对视图和特权信息（蛋白质位置/身份）指导架构设计，CARNIVAL有效弥合了模拟与真实数据之间的域差距，实现了跨域的自监督蛋白质注释，证明了模拟数据在cryo-ET蛋白质识别中的实用性。

Abstract: We explore the use of simulated data for training a model for protein annotation in crowded cryo-electron tomography volumes reconstructed from images collected at limited tilt angles and severely corrupted by the measurement operator. Firstly, we leverage the corruptions imposed by the forward model to generate domain-specific augmented paired views of the exact same scene for an invariance objective integrated into the LeJEPA self-supervised training framework. Secondly, we use additional information from the simulation pipeline such as the positions and identity of proteins in the simulated volumes to inform the architecture of the model and the loss function, so that semantic information is localised at protein positions in the resulting dense feature volume. The resulting model, CARNIVAL, is evaluated without finetuning on classification and detection tasks in real tomograms, using a benchmark dataset containing multiple protein types and two tomogram processing types. We show that CARNIVAL outperforms a state-of-the-art model trained using a contrastive objective on simulated data but without forward model-based paired views or privileged information.

</details>


### [113] [Object Concepts Emerge from Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04348)
*Boshi Li,Xiaohui Wang,Xiaoyang Wu,Zhichao Li,Ya Yang,Naiyan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个受生物学启发的框架，利用运动边界（光流+聚类生成伪实例掩码）为单图像编码器提供目标级分组监督，通过像素级成对度量学习训练，无需人工标注或相机标定。使用1.95亿到4.21亿帧伪标记数据训练Swin系列编码器，在单目深度估计、3D检测、3D占用预测和端到端规划等任务上达到或超越有监督与自监督预训练基线，尤其在地理和实例敏感任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉预训练方法通常捕获语义类别，但未能保留单个实例的身份与连贯性；目标中心化的视觉表征对物理世界感知至关重要，因此需要一种无需人类标注即可学习实例级对象表征的预训练方法。

Method: 1) 利用现成光流与聚类生成伪实例掩码作为对象级分组信号；2) 以伪掩码监督单图像编码器，进行像素级成对度量学习；3) 第一阶段在7,163小时（1.95亿帧）驾驶与网络视频上训练；4) 第二阶段引入Motion-Verified Self-Training，结合模型提议与运动证据，扩展监督至4.21亿帧；5) 训练至Swin-H规模并蒸馏至Swin骨干系列。

Result: 在单目深度估计、3D对象检测、3D占用预测和端到端规划任务中，模型相比有监督与自监督预训练基线取得竞争性或更优性能，尤其在地理/实例敏感任务上迁移表现突出。

Conclusion: 运动派生的监督能够教会静态图像编码器表征视觉实例，为大规模视觉预训练提供了与语义分类互补的新方向。

Abstract: Object-centric visual representations are important for physical-world perception, but existing visual pretraining methods often capture semantic categories without preserving the identity and coherence of individual instances. We present a biologically inspired framework that learns object-centric representations for single images from raw videos. Our approach uses motion boundaries as a source of object-level grouping: off-the-shelf optical flow and clustering produce pseudo-instance masks, which supervise a single-image encoder with pixel-level pairwise metric learning. The framework requires neither human annotations nor camera calibration. We first obtain 195 million pseudo-labeled frames from 7,163 hours of driving and web videos, then expand the supervision to 421 million frames with Motion-Verified Self-Training, which combines model proposals with motion evidence. We train encoders up to Swin-H and distill the learned representations into a family of Swin backbones. Across monocular depth estimation, 3D object detection, 3D occupancy prediction, and end-to-end planning, the resulting models achieve competitive or superior performance relative to supervised and self-supervised pretraining baselines, with particularly strong transfer on geometry- and instance-sensitive tasks. These results show that motion-derived supervision can teach static image encoders to represent visual instances, providing a complementary direction for scalable visual pretraining.

</details>


### [114] [AdaptVPR: Route-Aware Hard Positive Generation for Robust Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04369)
*Shunpeng Chen,Jingyi Zhang,Changwei Wang,Shengpeng Xu,Yukun Song,Xingtian Pei,Jinzhou Lin,Li Guo,Shibiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptVPR，一种路由感知的生成式增强框架，用于构建同一地点的难正样本以提升视觉地点识别（VPR）在领域偏移下的鲁棒性。框架结合视觉语言模型解析场景属性、规则调度器决定生成路线（全局外观、局部遮挡、双重路线），并通过几何一致性与外观多样性的VPR验证机制保证生成质量。由此构建了包含16万已验证合成难正样本的AdaptCities数据集，在多个VPR基线和视觉基础骨干上取得一致提升，R@1最高提升9.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VPR在许多标准基准上表现良好，但常受光照、天气、季节和动态遮挡等域偏移影响而退化。作者认为一个关键成因是现有训练数据中同一地点的外观多样性不足，因此需要一种能够生成同一地点不同外观的难正样本的方法来增强VPR训练的鲁棒性。

Method: AdaptVPR包含四个核心组件：(1) 视觉语言模型（VLM）解析场景属性并评估可编辑性；(2) 基于规则的调度器根据编辑性评分和风险约束确定生成路线；(3) 三条互补的生成路线——全局外观路线（天气/光照/时间变化）、局部遮挡路线（插入动态遮挡物）和双重路线（两种扰动组合）；(4) VPR导向的验证机制，基于几何一致性和外观多样性评估生成候选，全局候选一次性生成并拒绝不合格内容，局部遮挡和双重候选用验证反馈进行有限次提示细化与再生成。

Result: 构建了AdaptCities数据集，包含16万条已验证的合成同一地点难正样本。在多个VPR基线和视觉基础骨干上验证，标准基准上获得一致性能提升，在困难域偏移场景下R@1提升最高达9.2%。

Conclusion: AdaptVPR通过路由感知的生成式增强有效补充了训练数据中同一地点的外观多样性，为VPR提供了更具挑战性的难正样本。几何一致性与外观多样性的验证机制保障了生成质量，降低了结构漂移风险，实验证明了该方法在不同骨干网络和VPR算法上的普适性。

Abstract: Visual Place Recognition (VPR) localizes a query image by retrieving database images of the same or nearby place, yet its robustness is often degraded by domain shifts arising from illumination, weather, seasonal changes, and dynamic occlusions. One contributing factor is the limited appearance diversity of the same place in existing training data. To address this issue, we propose AdaptVPR, a route-aware generative augmentation framework that constructs same-place hard positives for robust VPR training. AdaptVPR first uses a vision language model to parse scene attributes and estimate editing feasibility, while a rule-based scheduler determines the generation route according to editability scores and risk constraints. The generation process is decomposed into three complementary routes: the Global Appearance Route introduces global scene changes in weather, illumination, and time of day; the Local Occlusion Route inserts plausible dynamic occluders; and the Dual Route combines both types of perturbations to produce more challenging appearance shifts. Each generated candidate is evaluated using a VPR-oriented verification scheme based on geometric consistency and appearance diversity, reducing the risk of structural drift while ensuring sufficient appearance variation. Global candidates are generated once and rejected if verification fails, while Local Occlusion and Dual candidates use verification feedback for limited prompt refinement and regeneration. Using this framework, we construct AdaptCities, containing 160K verified synthetic same-place hard positives. Experiments across multiple VPR baselines and vision foundation backbones show consistent gains on standard benchmarks and substantial improvements under challenging domain shifts, with R@1 gains of up to 9.2%. The source code and data resources are publicly available at https://github.com/chenshunpeng/AdaptVPR.

</details>


### [115] [Where Appearance Fails, Geometry Recognizes: A CAD-Free 3D Shape Prior That Complements Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04381)
*Chenxi Tao,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在无标注训练集且缺乏CAD模型的情况下，如何利用短时物体为中心的三维扫描（通过3D高斯泼溅3DGS重建）来提升特定物体的识别性能。核心发现是：3DGS重建的几何信息可替代CAD先验（精度相当），几何先验对形状独特物体增益显著、对低纹理相似工业件增益适度但一致，且该先验是互补性的（挽救图像失败多于破坏成功），其价值主要源于几何而非渲染像素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业与服务机器人常需在无标注训练集的情况下识别特定上线物体，但传统做法依赖CAD模型这类可渲染先验，而CAD模型经常缺失。纯2D图像没有形状先验，冻结的基础视觉特征（如DINOv2）又在几何相似、低纹理的工业零件上失效。作者希望探究：一次短时的物体为中心扫描，除了提供图像本身之外，还能为识别带来多少额外价值。

Method: 每个物体先用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行物体为中心的重建，归纳为每类形状原型，并与冻结的DINOv2图像特征进行融合。研究通过系统对比RGB-D深度、3DGS点云和CAD三种几何来源的识别效果，验证3DGS仅是获取点云的便捷途径；并分析几何先验在形状独特（HOPE）与形状混淆（T-LESS）两类数据集上的增益、互补性、遮挡鲁棒性以及光照不变性。

Result: 1) 扫描恢复了"无CAD"的CAD识别价值：RGB-D深度、3DGS与CAD三者的几何识别性能相当（HOPE上持平，T-LESS上差距在1.6分以内）。2) 增益取决于形状可辨识度：HOPE上几何单独达0.920（图像仅0.832），固定权重融合0.872低于几何上限；T-LESS上增益适中但一致（融合0.591，高于两种单信号）。3) 先验互补而非均匀叠加：挽救的图像失败远多于破坏的成功，且在部分遮挡下增益更大。4) 价值在几何而非渲染像素：3DGS渲染图不能帮助图像端，冻结特征识别几乎光照不变（差异在2.5分内）。

Conclusion: 短时物体扫描提供的几何信息能够在无CAD的情况下恢复CAD级的识别价值；该几何先验的价值取决于物体形状的可辨识程度，它是互补性的而非无条件叠加，且对遮挡鲁棒。其本质价值来源于几何结构本身而非3DGS的渲染像素。研究范围限定于识别任务，不涉及BOP位姿基准。

Abstract: Recognizing specific objects onboarded without a labeled training set recurs across manufacturing and service robotics, yet the conventional renderable prior, a computer-aided-design (CAD) model, is often unavailable. Two-dimensional capture supplies no shape prior, and frozen foundation features fail on geometrically similar, low-texture industrial parts. We ask what a short object-centric scan buys for recognition beyond the captured images themselves: each object is reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), summarized into a per-class shape prototype, and fused with frozen DINOv2 image features. First, the scan recovers the recognition value of CAD without CAD: geometry from RGB-D depth (on T-LESS), 3DGS, and CAD gives comparable recognition (tied on HOPE, within 1.6 points on T-LESS); 3DGS is only a convenient route to a point cloud. Second, the payoff is governed by how recognizable the shape is: on shape-distinctive household objects (HOPE) geometry alone reaches 0.920 versus image-only 0.832, a ceiling below which fixed-weight fusion (0.872) sits.
