Abstract: Chilli (Capsicum annuum) is one of India's most economically significant crops, yet its productivity is persistently threatened by diseases that are difficult to identify without expert intervention. While Vision Transformers (ViTs) have achieved high classification accuracy, their large computational footprint makes deployment on resource constrained devices challenging. Existing compression approaches typically address pruning, quantization, and knowledge distillation in isolation, leaving the potential benefits and interactions of their combined application insufficiently explored. We propose a unified Vision Transformer compression framework that combines Hessian-Balanced Adaptive Block Pruning (H-BAC), guided by second-order sensitivity estimation, with quantization and attention-based knowledge distillation. To systematically identify the most effective configuration within each compression family, each technique is first evaluated independently through controlled ablation studies, after which the best-performing components are integrated into a sequential deployment pipeline tailored to real-world agricultural constraints. On a chilli 3-class village-split dataset with a genuine cross-village, cross-device out-of-distribution test split, the resulting compressed models match or exceed the 95.13% FP32 baseline's accuracy, alongside 74-98% model size reduction, and the fully integrated compression pipeline achieves a 54.5x size reduction (327.42 MB to 6.01 MB) at 95.13 +/- 2.32% accuracy across four tested configurations. A direct comparison further reveals that, on this dataset, a directly-trained student of the same final size, without pruning or distillation, reaches comparable accuracy of 94.87%, at the same 6.01 MB INT8 size, indicating where H-BAC and knowledge distillation are, and are not yet shown to be, worth their computational cost.

</details>


### [181] [MEOX: Compact Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05351)
*Mohanad Albughdadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEOX是一个仅293.9万参数编码器、总计311.5万参数的轻量级多模态掩码自编码器，通过传感器专用适配器、显式有效性信号与共享稀疏专家块实现多模态地球观测表示学习。在122.8万MMEarth64样本上预训练后，在GEO-Bench六项任务上冻结迁移表现优异（cashew分割64像素mIoU 64.42%，EuroSAT 224像素平均准确率90.56%），BigEarthNet微调微平均精度达72.95%，超越对应CSMoE结果，展示了紧凑参数预算下传感器灵活表示学习与强任务迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测表示学习方法通常通过扩展架构规模来应对异质传感器与缺失观测问题，但参数开销巨大。研究者希望探索一种更紧凑、参数效率更高的多模态学习方案，在保持跨传感器表示学习能力的同时，显著降低架构复杂度与计算预算。

Method: 采用多模态掩码自编码器架构，包含：(1) 传感器专用适配器与显式有效性信号，保留模态特定处理；(2) 共享稀疏专家块配合私有低秩残差约束参数增长；(3) 四元元数据令牌与单一空间序列共同通过后续十四层编码器；(4) 旋转注意力支持与预训练不同的下游空间网格；(5) 在122.8万MMEarth64样本上以模态平衡掩码重建与结构化传感器丢失进行预训练。

Result: 在六个GEO-Bench任务上以64和224像素两种分辨率进行冻结迁移评估：cashew分割在64像素达64.42% mean IoU，EuroSAT在224像素达90.56%平均准确率，均超过报告的CSMoE对应结果；BigEarthNet微调达到72.95%微平均精度；路由诊断区分了专家参与度、空间依赖、模态关联与功能贡献；WorldCover探测显示元数据带来0.64个百分点增益；检索实验区分了同传感器语义与跨传感器对齐。

Conclusion: MEOX证明可以通过精巧的架构设计（稀疏专家、专用适配器、元数据令牌、低秩残差）而非盲目扩大参数规模来实现传感器灵活的多模态地球观测表示学习。紧凑参数预算下的强任务迁移和检索/路由诊断结果验证了其有效性与可解释性，为资源受限场景下的遥感表示学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in Earth Observation representation learning accommodate heterogeneous sensors and missing observations, often through larger architectures. We present MEOX (Multimodal Earth Observation with eXperts), a multimodal masked autoencoder with a 2.939 million-parameter encoder and 3.115 million parameters in total. Sensor-specific adapters, explicit validity signals, and a shared sparse-expert block preserve modality-dependent processing before a learned patch-wise fusion. Four metadata tokens then accompany a single spatial sequence through fourteen further encoder blocks. Shared expert projections with private low-rank residuals constrain parameter growth, while rotary attention supports downstream spatial grids different from pretraining. The model is pretrained on 1.228 million MMEarth64 samples using modality-balanced masked reconstruction and structured sensor dropout. Frozen transfer is evaluated on six GEO-Bench tasks at both 64 and 224 pixels. The model reaches 64.42% mean intersection-over-union on cashew segmentation at 64 pixels and 90.56% average accuracy on EuroSAT at 224 pixels, exceeding the corresponding reported CSMoE results. BigEarthNet finetuning reaches 72.95% micro-average precision. Routing diagnostics distinguish expert participation, spatial dependence, modality association, and functional contribution. A held-out WorldCover probe measures a 0.64-percentage-point benefit from metadata, while retrieval separates same-sensor semantics from cross-sensor alignment. These results demonstrate sensor-flexible representation learning and strong task transfer using a compact parameter budget.

</details>


### [182] [Reflection-aware Generative Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05382)
*GeonU Kim,Shin Dong-Yeon,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ref-GeNVS 是一种免训练的、感知反射的生成式新视角合成方法，专门针对镜子场景。其核心思想是将镜中影像视为互补视图：先从输入图像中估计镜面平面并反射相机位姿形成虚拟视图，再通过 Mirror-gated attention 和 Reflection injection 两阶段生成机制，显式利用多视角扩散模型中的反射关系，实现反射一致的新视角合成，无需微调即可继承多视角扩散骨干的强大泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多视角扩散模型在镜子场景中往往无法识别镜子，也无法利用反射内容进行场景生成，导致生成的新视角中反射不一致或不完整。

Method: 无需额外训练，核心思路是将镜中图像视为两个互补视角。流程包括：①从输入图像估计镜面平面并反射相机位姿形成虚拟视图；②提出两阶段生成方法：Mirror-gated attention（镜子门控注意力）与 Reflection injection（反射注入），在多视角扩散模型中显式利用反射关系，实现反射一致的NVS。

Result: 在包含镜子的合成场景和真实场景上，Ref-GeNVS 优于近期生成式NVS方法，能够生成反射一致且上下文连贯的新视角，揭示仅能通过镜子看到的场景结构。

Conclusion: Ref-GeNVS 作为一种免训练方法，可通过显式建模镜子反射关系提升生成式NVS在镜子场景中的表现，同时保留多视角扩散骨干的强泛化能力，无需微调。

Abstract: We propose Ref-GeNVS, a training-free, reflection-aware method for generative novel view synthesis (NVS) in mirror scenes. Existing multi-view diffusion models often fail to recognize the mirror in the scene and cannot exploit reflected content for scene generation. To fix this issue without additional training, our key idea is to treat a mirror image as two complementary views. From input images, we estimate the mirror plane and reflect camera poses to form virtual views. Based on this virtual view setup, we propose a two-stage generation method consisting of Mirror-gated attention and Reflection injection, which enables reflection-consistent NVS by explicitly leveraging reflection relationships in a multi-view diffusion model. Ref-GeNVS inherits the strong generalizability of the multi-view diffusion backbone, while it does not require finetuning. On synthetic and real scenes including mirrors, Ref-GeNVS outperforms recent generative NVS methods by generating reflection-consistent and contextually coherent novel views, revealing scene structure visible only through mirrors. Project page: https://kim-geonu.github.io/Ref-GeNVS/

</details>


### [183] [Think-Verify-Revise: Neuro-Symbolic Visual Reasoning with Vision-Language Models and Dynamic Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05388)
*Homayoun Afshari,Pietro Basci,Alessandro Russo,Lia Morra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号(NeSy)框架，通过将视觉语言模型(VLM)用于自动一阶逻辑(FOL)规则归纳，与动态逻辑张量网络(D-LTN)进行可微规则验证闭环迭代，在ViSudo-PC基准上仅用三个训练样例即可在四个视觉域中归纳出有效的数独约束规则，AUC成绩匹配或超越NeuPSL与LTN等既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉推理任务要求系统同时感知视觉内容并应用形式化关系约束，但纯神经方法缺乏符号推理能力，纯符号方法又难以处理原始视觉输入，两者单独处理均效果欠佳，需要弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 构建Think-Verify-Revise闭环迭代框架：VLM接收少量带标签视觉样例提出符合严格语法的候选FOL规则(Think)；依据这些规则在运行时自动组装D-LTN，基于CNN生成的视觉嵌入进行可微规则验证(Verify)；验证失败信息反馈指导VLM生成下一假设(Revise)。

Result: 在ViSudo-PC基准上横跨MNIST、EMNIST、KMNIST、FMNIST四个视觉域进行评测，系统仅用三个训练样例作为视觉上下文即可归纳出有效的数独约束规则，AUC分数匹配或超越NeuPSL与LTN等前人方法。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了通过VLM进行自动规则发现（而非人工手动设计规则）在神经符号推理中的潜力，为视觉关系推理提供了纯神经与纯符号方法之外的有效统一路径。

Abstract: Visual reasoning tasks require a system to jointly perceive visual content and apply formal relational constraints---a combination that neither pure neural nor purely symbolic approaches handle well in isolation. This paper proposes a Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) framework that closes this gap by tightly coupling a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for automatic First-Order Logic (FOL) rule induction with a Dynamic Logic Tensor Network (D-LTN) for differentiable rule verification, in a closed iterative feedback loop. The VLM receives a small set of labelled visual examples and proposes candidate FOL rules conforming to a strict grammar (Think); the D-LTN is automatically assembled from these rules at runtime and evaluates them grounding on CNN-produced visual embeddings (Verify); and verification failures are fed back to guide the VLM's next hypothesis (Revise). Evaluated on the ViSudo-PC benchmark across four visual domains (MNIST, EMNIST, KMNIST, FMNIST), the system induces valid Sudoku constraint rules using only three training examples as visual context. The proposed method achieves AUC scores matching or outperforming previous methods (NeuPSL, LTN), showing the potential for automatic rule discovery through VLM. Code is available at https://github.com/homayoun-afshari/nesy.

</details>


### [184] [CrossDepth: Geometry-Constrained Attention for Generalizable Multi-View Surround Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05397)
*Samer Abualhanud,Max Mehltretter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督多视角环绕相机深度估计方法，通过逐像素相机感知射线嵌入处理相机内参差异，并通过几何约束的跨图像注意力扩展感受野，从而提升跨图像深度一致性与整体精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要对周围环境进行可靠的3D理解。多视角环绕相机系统虽能提供广阔场景覆盖，但相邻图像重叠极少，大多数像素的深度必须依靠单目外观线索推断。这些线索在不同图像间表现可能不同，导致深度估计模型产生跨图像不一致的结果。作者识别出两大不一致来源：相机内参差异以及每幅图像有限的感受野。

Method: 针对相机内参差异，在特征上引入逐像素的相机感知射线嵌入（camera-aware ray embeddings），使网络能感知相机相关的单目线索变化。针对感受野有限问题，通过受几何合理区域约束的跨图像注意力机制扩展每个像素的上下文信息，该约束源自标定好的环绕相机系统布局。模型以光度一致性为准则进行完全自监督训练。

Result: 在DDAD和nuScenes数据集上，与最先进的自监督方法相比，在域内与跨域评估下均取得了更高的整体深度精度和更好的跨图像深度一致性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模相机内参与跨图像几何约束，该方法有效缓解了多视角环绕相机深度估计中的跨图像不一致问题，在自监督框架下实现了优于现有方法的深度精度与一致性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Reliable 3D understanding of the surrounding environment is a core requirement for autonomous driving. Multi-view surround camera rigs provide broad scene coverage, but the spatially adjacent images typically overlap only minimally. Consequently, the depth of most pixels must be inferred from monocular appearance cues. These cues can appear differently across images and may therefore be interpreted differently by the depth estimation model. We target two main sources of cross-image inconsistency: differences in camera intrinsics and the limited receptive field of each image. We address the former by conditioning the features on per-pixel camera-aware ray embeddings, enabling the network to account for camera-dependent variations in monocular cues. We address the latter by extending each pixel's context beyond its own image through cross-image attention constrained to geometrically plausible regions, derived from the calibrated rig setup. The model is trained in a fully self-supervised manner based on photometric consistency. Evaluations on DDAD and nuScenes show improved overall depth accuracy and cross-image depth consistency over state-of-the-art self-supervised methods under in-domain and cross-domain evaluation. Code is available at https://abualhanud.github.io/CrossDepthPage/.

</details>


### [185] [From Interpretability Methods to Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05399)
*Julien Colin,Nuria Oliver,Thomas Serre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文主张将可解释AI（XAI）领域的研究重心从"构建和比较解释方法"转向"以模型为中心"的研究范式，即使用现有成熟的XAI工具来表征、比较和理解模型本身的可解释性，并强调"模型能否被人类实际理解"必须通过独立评估者测量而非推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十余年，视觉领域的XAI已积累了丰富的工具（归因、特征可视化、概念方法、电路方法），但几乎全部精力都花在构建和比较这些方法上，而忽略了这些方法最初要回答的核心问题——模型的可解释性如何？随着模型演进，可解释性是否在进步？

Method: 论文通过文献综述和论证的方式展开：(1)论证现有XAI工具已足够成熟，可支撑"以模型为中心"的特征化与比较；(2)梳理现有的模型对比研究（thin body of work）；(3)与系统神经科学进行类比，借鉴其研究范式；(4)提出以模型为中心的XAI研究议程。

Result: 论文通过梳理指出：模型间的表征与计算差异可以用现有工具进行刻画和比较（这是可实现的）；但"模型能否被依赖它的人类真正理解"这一更难的问题被严重忽视，其必须经由独立评估者（而非自证预期的专家）进行测量，不能仅靠推断。作者提出模型为中心的XAI研究议程，涵盖两条互补路线。

Conclusion: XAI领域应当实现从方法中心到模型中心的范式转移。现有工具已能支持对模型的表征与计算进行系统比较；而模型的可理解性必须作为可测量的属性，借助独立人类评估者加以实证评估。这一转变将帮助领域回答其最初的核心科学问题，并促进可信认证与模型演化中的可解释性追踪。

Abstract: More than a decade in, explainable AI (XAI) for computer vision has assembled a mature toolbox: attribution, feature visualization, concept-based, and circuit-based methods. Yet almost all of the field's effort has gone into building and comparing these methods, and little into the question they were meant to answer---how interpretable are our models, and are we making progress as they evolve? We argue for shifting the field's focus from methods to models, along two complementary lines. One is already within reach: existing tools let us characterize and compare what different models represent and compute. The other is harder, and largely neglected: whether a model can actually be understood by the humans who rely on it---the independent evaluators on whom trust and certification depend, not the experts confirming what they already expect. It can only be measured, not inferred. We review why the toolbox is mature enough to support both, survey the thin body of work comparing models, draw a parallel to systems neuroscience, and close with a model-centric XAI agenda.

</details>


### [186] [A Generalizable Feature Extractor for Alzheimer's-Related Brain MRI Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05400)
*Reza Rajabli,D. Louis Collins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了一个在脑龄预测任务上训练的紧凑型3D CNN（约718万参数，冻结权重）能否作为阿尔茨海默病相关下游神经影像任务的通用基础模型。通过仅使用约1%的可训练参数（LoRA低秩适配）进行任务适配，并在六个实验中验证其泛化能力：ADNI数据集痴呆分类AUC 0.964；无需再训练直接迁移到OASIS-3得到AUC 0.871；结合认知评分区分稳定性与进展性MCI的AUC 0.828；从结构MRI预测淀粉样蛋白阳性AUC 0.804；以及通过T1加权图像直接估计海马体和白质高信号体积（R²分别达0.80和0.91）。结果表明，精心训练的脑龄模型可以作为阿尔茨海默相关任务的有效基础模型，即使在严格数据约束下也能工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习在神经影像领域尤其是阿尔茨海默病研究中的有效性尚未被充分理解，且不清楚预训练模型能否在不同数据集和任务间无需重新训练即可迁移。作者希望验证一个紧凑的有监督预训练模型能否作为可复用的基础模型，以应对标注数据不足的困境。

Method: 采用先前在脑龄预测任务上训练的3D CNN作为骨干网络并冻结其718万参数权重，使用低秩适配（LoRA）技术仅引入约1%的可训练参数来适配各下游任务。设计了六个泛化实验，涵盖不同数据集（ADNI、OASIS-3）和不同任务（痴呆分类、MCI进展预测、淀粉样蛋白阳性预测、体积回归）。

Result: 六个实验均取得良好性能：ADNI上认知正常vs痴呆分类AUC 0.964；同模型不经再训练直接应用于OASIS-3获AUC 0.871；结合logit与年龄、认知评分区分稳定/进展性MCI的AUC为0.828；从结构MRI预测淀粉样蛋白阳性AUC 0.804；ICV归一化的海马体积和脑白质高信号体积估计R²分别为0.80和0.91（通常需要更大规模U-Net的回归任务）。

Conclusion: 一个在脑龄上监督训练的紧凑模型可以作为阿尔茨海默病相关任务的通用可重用骨干，能够以约1%的可训练参数适配多种任务，并能零训练迁移到未见过的队列。这一发现表明，即使数据极度受限，精心训练的脑龄模型仍可成为有效的医学影像基础模型。

Abstract: When there is not enough labeled data to properly train deep learning models, transfer learning can help. We still do not fully understand how effective it is in neuroimaging, especially for Alzheimer's disease research. It is also not clear if these transferred models can work on new datasets without being retrained for each specific task. We evaluate whether a compact, supervised pretrained model can serve as a reusable foundation model for downstream neuroimaging tasks. We freeze the 7.18 million weights of a 3D CNN previously trained for brain-age prediction, and adapt it to each task using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), requiring only ~1% additional trainable parameters. We evaluate generalizability in six experiments. Adapting the model to classify cognitively normal versus Dementia on ADNI gave an AUC of 0.964 on held-out folds (Experiment #1). Applying that adapted model unchanged to OASIS-3, with no retraining, gave an AUC of 0.871 (Experiment #2). Reusing its output logit together with age and a cognitive score distinguished stable from progressing MCI with an AUC of 0.828 (Experiment #3). Adapting the same backbone to predict amyloid positivity from structural MRI gave an AUC of 0.804 (Experiment #4). Finally, the same approach estimated ICV-normalized hippocampal and white matter hypointensity volumes directly from the T1w image, with R^2 of 0.80 and 0.91 respectively, tasks normally addressed with much larger U-Net networks (Experiments #5 and #6). A compact model supervised on brain age can therefore serve as a reusable backbone, adapting to each task with ~1% additional parameters and transferring to an unseen cohort without any training. Our findings suggest that a carefully trained brain age model can serve as an effective foundation model for Alzheimer's related tasks, even under strict data constraints.

</details>


### [187] [UniMate: One Unified Model to Animate Diverse Skeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05415)
*Linzhan Mou,Jiahui Lei,Zhiyang Dou,Chenyue Cai,Chaoyue Song,Adam Finkelstein,Szymon Rusinkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniMate是一个统一的基础模型，能够仅从带骨骼绑定的3D资产和文本提示词生成任意骨架的关节运动，无需测试时优化或逐骨架重训练。通过拓扑感知的扩散Transformer与专用数据集UniML3D，实现了跨拓扑的零样本运动合成与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动绑定（rigging）技术已能大规模产出可供动画的3D资产，但生成驱动这些资产的运动仍是一大瓶颈。现有学习方法受限于拓扑结构：要么依赖类别特定的模板，要么在推理时需要逐骨架微调和参考运动，这严重束缚了运动生成的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了拓扑感知的扩散Transformer，通过三种机制将骨骼拓扑集成到注意力中：(1) 基于成对关节关系与测地距离的图感知注意力偏置；(2) 通过图拉普拉斯将RoPE推广到任意运动学树的谱旋转位置编码；(3) 对静态骨架进行注意力池化的全局拓扑条件器。同时构建了UniML3D数据集，包含13,006个运动序列，涵盖双足、四足、鸟类、海洋生物、昆虫类、蛇形及带关节的刚体对象，并进行了统一的规范化与文本配对。

Result: 在该数据集上训练后，UniMate在质量、泛化性和效率上均优于最先进的基线方法，并支持零样本跨拓扑迁移、中间帧插值、运动扩展以及文本引导的运动编辑。

Conclusion: UniMate展示了从任意绑定骨骼与文本直接合成关节化运动是可行的，为摆脱拓扑约束的通用运动生成提供了统一框架，可作为该领域的基础模型方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in automatic rigging now deliver animation-ready 3D assets at scale, yet generating the motion to drive them remains a bottleneck. Existing learned animators are topology-constrained: they rely on category-specific templates or require per-skeleton fine-tuning and reference motions at inference. We present UniMate, a unified foundation model that synthesizes articulated motion for arbitrary skeletons from a rigged 3D asset and a text prompt, with no test-time optimization or per-skeleton retraining. UniMate introduces a topology-aware diffusion transformer, which integrates skeletal topology into attention via three mechanisms: (1) a graph-aware attention bias from pairwise joint relations and geodesic distances; (2) a spectral rotary position embedding generalizing RoPE to arbitrary kinematic trees via the graph Laplacian; and (3) a global topological conditioner attention-pooled from the rest-pose skeleton. We also curate UniML3D, 13,006 motion sequences spanning bipedal, quadrupedal, avian, marine, insectoid, serpentine, and articulated rigid objects with unified canonicalization and text pairing. Trained on this dataset, UniMate outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in quality, generalization, and efficiency, and supports zero-shot cross-topology transfer, in-betweening, expansion, and text-guided editing. Our project page is available at https://linzhanmou.com/unimate/.

</details>


### [188] [WorldSculpt: Generating Compositional Worlds from Grounded Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05416)
*Muyao Niu,Jixuan He,Ruihan Yu,Lian Fu,Yonghao Yu,Zheng-Hui Huang,Yifan Zhan,Fengbo Lan,Yongtao Ge,Yinqiang Zheng,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将强单物体3D生成先验通过多视角条件路径适配到多视角观测，实现包含数百个物体的杂乱场景的合成式3D网格重建。方法仅用单物体规范空间微调，无需场景级训练即可泛化到严重遮挡的大场景，并引入UE-MeshyScene基准及跨多种场景的评估，表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下游应用（游戏、AR/VR、仿真、机器人）需要将杂乱场景表示为共享世界坐标系下的独立物体网格集合。现有几何方法通常将场景重建为单一表示，在遮挡区域留下不完整几何；而带生成先验的合成方法大多局限于较简单的场景。

Method: 采用Pixal3D作为基础模型，扩展其多视角调节通路，使物体生成基于多个有姿态的观测。模型仅在单物体的规范空间中微调，但在无需场景级训练的情况下泛化到严重遮挡的大场景，实现合成式场景生成。

Result: 在单物体、受控多物体、UE-MeshyScene评估中，方法均优于先前方法，且场景复杂度和遮挡增加时优势更大。此外，能将从3DGS世界（如Marble和HY-World 2.0）生成的场景转换为合成式网格场景。

Conclusion: 通过适配单物体3D生成先验到多视角观测，该范式被证明在处理包含数百个带严重遮挡物体的复杂场景时是可行且可扩展的，展现了对大规模合成式3D场景重建的潜力。

Abstract: We study the problem of generating a compositional 3D representation of a cluttered scene containing hundreds of objects. The goal is to represent the scene as a collection of individual object meshes placed in a shared world frame, as required by downstream applications such as gaming, AR/VR, simulation, and robotics. This task is challenging in densely cluttered scenes, where objects heavily occlude one another and each view reveals only a fraction of their geometry. Geometry-based approaches typically reconstruct the scene as a single representation and leave incomplete geometry in occluded regions, while existing compositional methods with generative priors are largely limited to relatively simple scenes. We show that complex scenes with hundreds of objects can instead be generated compositionally by adapting a strong single-object 3D generative prior to multi-view observations. We instantiate this paradigm with Pixal3D, extending it with a multi-view conditioning pathway that grounds object generation in multiple posed observations. Although the model is finetuned entirely on single objects in canonical space, it generalizes to large scenes with severe occlusion without any scene-level training, demonstrating the feasibility and scalability of this paradigm. We further introduce UE-MeshyScene, a photorealistic benchmark of densely cluttered scenes with hundreds of objects, per-object annotations, and ground-truth meshes. Across single-object, controlled multi-object, and UE-MeshyScene evaluations, our method consistently outperforms prior approaches, with larger gains as scene complexity and occlusion increase. Finally, we demonstrate broader applicability by converting generated 3DGS worlds, such as Marble and HY-World 2.0, into compositional mesh scenes.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Blockchain-Enabled Secure Logging for Fiscal Electronic Mechanisms: Evaluation of the Greek eSEND and myDATA Tax Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04356)
*Panagiotis Mavridis,Anargyros Baklezos,Christos Nikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了希腊两种税务系统的完整性机制：基于区块链的FEM硬件设备（电子收银机、税务打印机等）与eSEND中央系统，以及不含区块链的电子发票供应商服务与myDATA平台，结论是硬件型税务机制在交易完整性和防篡改方面优于纯软件型发票基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字税务生态系统中，交易完整性和防篡改能力是核心安全需求。希腊同时存在基于区块链的硬件税务机制与纯软件的电子发票体系，两者在完整性保障上存在明显架构差异，需要对这两种路径进行系统比较以识别当前电子发票框架的设计弱点。

Method: 对希腊财政电子机制（FEMs）与eSEND中央税务信息系统进行密码学架构分析，考察其双重或三重哈希链结构、传输协议的加密与顺序验证机制；同时对比电子发票供应商服务与myDATA中央平台的架构，评估其区块链完整性保障的缺失情况，并进行系统性比较分析。

Result: 研究发现，基于硬件的财政机制通过区块链与多重哈希链为交易完整性提供了更强的保障，而纯软件化的电子发票架构缺乏同等的区块链级完整性保证；比较结果揭示了当前电子发票框架在交易完整性保障上的架构性弱点。

Conclusion: 硬件型区块链税务机制优于纯软件型发票基础设施，当前数字税务生态系统中的电子发票框架存在完整性保障缺陷，需要在此基础上提出架构改进方案以强化交易完整性。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the implementation of blockchain-based integrity mechanisms in Greek Fiscal Electronic Mechanisms (FEMs) and the central tax information system eSEND. The study examines the cryptographic architecture of fiscal devices, including Electronic Cash Registers, Fiscal Printers, Fiscal Signing Machines, and FEMAS devices, which implement double or triple hash-chain structures to ensure transaction immutability. The transmission protocol between fiscal devices and the central database is also evaluated with respect to encryption, sequential validation, and blockchain verification. In contrast, the architecture of Electronic Invoicing Provider Services and the myDATA central platform is analyzed, highlighting the absence of blockchain-based integrity guarantees. The comparison demonstrates that hardware-based fiscal mechanisms provide stronger guarantees for transaction completeness and tamper resistance than purely software-based invoicing infrastructures. The findings highlight architectural weaknesses in the current e-invoicing framework and propose improvements for ensuring transaction integrity in digital tax ecosystems.

</details>


### [190] [Privacy Failure in Split-LLM Training, The Returned Gradient Nullifies the Decoys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04382)
*Georgios Politis,Evangelos Pappas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对一种两节点拆分LLM训练系统进行了系统安全案例研究，发现其隐私评估虽通过但遗留了未测试的可观测信道。TLN向UCN发送受保护激活，UCN返回输出，TLN持有私密损失并返回输出梯度。UCN接收的帧混合真实行与诱饵行，损失忽略诱饵，其梯度恰好为零，因此零模式泄露了真实行。实验用固定协议（已知强度注入泄漏、打乱标签对照、预置阈值）验证：9个种子下每个帧的4096/4096行全部被识别；帧内容攻击比常数猜测基线每百个token多恢复约一个（+0.65至+1.50个百分点），打乱对照无恢复。在保持模型质量在预算内的配置上重复验证，两种数据集下所有运行都通过了前向信道隐私检查和质量检查，但加入返回梯度后全部失败。对梯度逐行裁剪加噪以约0.01 nats的交叉熵代价封堵了泄漏，但系统仍不安全：五类攻击（包括跨训练步累积观测）从未被测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示拆分LLM训练系统中隐私评估的盲区——即使前向信道隐私检查通过，返回梯度中仍可能遗留未测试的可观测信道（零梯度模式），从而泄露受保护数据的真实行信息。作者旨在指出现有检查协议的不完备性并量化该泄漏的实际风险。

Method: 采用固定协议测量泄漏：以已知强度注入泄漏证明仪器可检出；打乱标签作为对照证明不误报；事先设定阈值。在9个种子上，对比帧内容攻击与常数猜测基线的token恢复率，并在两种数据集上进行前向信道与质量检查，随后加入返回梯度重测。最后用逐行裁剪加噪作为防御，并检验其封堵效果与质量代价。

Result: 零梯度模式在每帧4,096行中全部（4,096/4,096）识别出真实行；帧内容攻击每百token多恢复约一个token（+0.65至+1.50个百分点），对照无恢复；两种数据集下所有运行均通过前向隐私与质量检查但加入梯度后全部失败。逐行裁剪加噪以约0.01 nats交叉熵代价封堵泄漏。

Conclusion: 该系统存在真实可利用的梯度侧信道，即使前向隐私评估通过也无法保证整体安全；虽然可低成本缓解，但仍有五类攻击（含跨步累积观测）未经测量，系统不能视为安全。

Abstract: We present a systems-security case study of a two-node split-LLM training system whose privacy evaluation passed while leaving an observable channel untested. The Trusted Local Node (TLN) sends protected activations to the Untrusted Cloud Node (UCN), the UCN returns its output, and TLN, holding the private loss, returns the output gradient. The frame the UCN receives mixes real rows with decoys, and the loss ignores the decoys. Their gradients are exactly zero, so the pattern of zeros reveals which rows were real. We measure it with a protocol fixed in advance: a leak injected at known strength to prove the instrument can see one, a shuffled-label control to prove it does not report absent leaks, and a threshold set before the runs. Across nine seeds, the zeros identified the real rows on every frame, 4,096 of 4,096 per run. An attack on the frame contents recovered about one extra token per hundred over a constant-guess baseline (+0.65 to +1.50 percentage points); the shuffled controls recovered nothing. A second set of runs repeated this on a configuration that keeps model quality within budget, so the finding is not confined to a setting nobody would deploy. On both datasets, every such run passed the forward-channel privacy check and the quality check, yet failed that same check once the returned gradient was included. Clipping and noising each row of the gradient closed the leak for about 0.01 nats of held-out cross-entropy. The system is not thereby safe: five classes of attack, including those accumulating observations across training steps, were never measured.

</details>


### [191] [Candidate Comparability Before Promotion: Conditional Validation in Adaptive Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04388)
*Roberto Fernández-Barrios,Iker Pastor-López,Amaia Pikatza-Huerga,Pablo García Bringas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应网络入侵检测系统中分类器"晋升(promotion)"决策的方法论问题——即漂移告警后挑战者分类器是否应替代已部署的现有分类器。作者发现晋升结论高度依赖挑战者的构建方式与支撑证据量，并通过三个基准数据集、多种更新策略与敏感性分析揭示：自带完整数据处理管线的挑战者可消除"现有分类器冻结预处理"带来的表面晋升危害；提高候选证据量能显著改善晋升（但效果因基准而异）；验证对证据劣势的挑战者有帮助但对等证据量时无平均增益。最终建议应显式控制、报告和解释挑战者构建与证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应网络入侵检测系统通常会在漂移告警后重训分类器，但告警只表明检测到了变化，并不能证明挑战者分类器应当替换已部署的现有分类器。晋升是安全攸关的（它会改变后续攻击检测的责任模型），而其评估存在方法论问题：晋升结论可能取决于挑战者如何构造以及支撑其的证据量大小。作者旨在检验这种依赖性并给出可靠的评估建议。

Method: 在CICIDS2017、UNSW-NB15和ToN-IoT三个数据集上进行系统性实验：采用自包含的完整挑战者数据处理管线（避免数据泄漏），嵌套的候选规模控制（证据量从512到2000样本/类），通过统一测试框架比较九种更新策略，并进行最终敏感性分析（确保每个精确特征向量只用于一种角色：训练或探测）。通过多次真实、时间有序流量的重放实验验证"始终部署"策略是否产生净危害。

Result: （1）现有分类器拥有的冻结预处理会放大表面上的晋升危害；改为自包含挑战者管线后，平均全漂移危害不再持续。（2）候选证据从512提升到2000样本/类，在池构建渐进漂移下晋升分别提升+0.53、+1.67和+0.38的平衡准确率百分点，三个基准均正向且统计显著，但材料性依赖基准而非均匀改善，主要由误报减少驱动。（3）策略结论仅部分稳健：策略排序随候选可比性变化，没有策略全局占优，先前对无标签估计器和校准集成的兼容性结论被收窄。（4）验证对证据劣势挑战者有帮助，但在证据等量时无平均增益。（5）十三次真实时间有序流量重放显示始终部署无净危害。

Conclusion: 晋升评估中的挑战者构造和证据量必须被显式控制、报告和解释。研究揭示了先前文献中因数据处理泄漏与候选规模设置不当可能造成的误导性结论，并强调晋升结论具有基准依赖性，需要更谨慎、标准化的评估框架来指导安全攸关的模型替换决策。

Abstract: Adaptive network intrusion detection systems retrain classifiers after drift alarms, but an alarm detects change; it does not establish that a challenger should replace the deployed incumbent. Promotion is security-relevant because it changes the model responsible for subsequent attack detection, and evaluating it has a methodological problem: promotion conclusions may depend on how the challenger was constructed and on how much evidence supports it. We test that dependence on CICIDS2017, UNSW-NB15 and ToN-IoT with self-contained challenger pipelines, nested candidate-size controls, a common-harness comparison of nine update policies, and a final sensitivity confining every exact feature vector to one evaluation, training or probe role. Incumbent-owned frozen preprocessing amplified apparent promotion harm; with self-contained challenger pipelines the mean full-drift harm did not persist. Raising nominal candidate evidence from 512 to 2,000 samples per class improved promotion under pool-constructed progressive drift by +0.53, +1.67 and +0.38 balanced-accuracy points: positive and statistically resolved in all three benchmarks, but materially benchmark-dependent rather than homogeneous, and driven mainly by fewer false positives. Policy conclusions were partially robust: policy ordering changed with candidate comparability, no policy globally dominated, and earlier compatibility statements for a label-free estimator and a calibrated ensemble narrowed. Validation helped evidence-disadvantaged challengers but added no average benefit at parity. Thirteen replays on real, time-ordered traffic showed no net harm from always deploying. Challenger construction and evidence should be controlled, reported and interpreted explicitly when promotion is evaluated.

</details>


### [192] [Engineered Persuasion: Evaluating Personalized Pretexts in LLM-Generated Spear Phishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04410)
*Jerson Francia,Derek Hansen,Benjamin Schooley,Shydra Valynn Murray*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究招募180名美国在职成年人评估AI生成的仿真钓鱼邮件，发现随着个性化程度逐级增加（从工作场所到同事/共享项目背景），邮件的可信度和点击意图均上升（每级分别增加2.40分和28%）。但细节的质量比数量更重要：与收件人角色和日常工作匹配的细节增强可信度，而不准确、模糊或渠道不当的细节反而引发怀疑。研究强调个性化效果取决于借口是否符合收件人的工作情境，并讨论了该发现对职场网络安全培训的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可以低成本地将工作场所细节注入钓鱼邮件借口，但这些细节可能增强或削弱消息的可信度。现有研究对个性化细节如何影响钓鱼邮件说服效果的理解尚不充分，需要系统探究个性化程度与细节质量的作用。

Method: 招募180名美国在职成年人参与公开调查，评估AI生成的仿真钓鱼邮件。实验采用四级累积信息设计（工作场所→收件人姓名与职位→工作职责→同事/共享项目背景），参与者对每封邮件0-100评分可信度、选择行动（打开链接、调查、删除或举报），并解释最高和最低评分邮件的原因。收集1,436条有效评估进行敏感性分析，并进行事后描述性分析与定性编码。

Result: 可信度每级个性化提升2.40分，表达点击意图的几率每级增加28%。未表达点击意图的参与者中，调查行为仍常见，举报减少，删除增加。事后分析发现，来自具名人物并提及提供同事信息的邮件比来自部门或实体的邮件获得更高评分和点击意图。定性编码显示，与参与者角色和工作常规匹配的细节支持可信度，而错误、模糊或渠道不当的细节引发怀疑。

Conclusion: 钓鱼邮件的个性化并非简单添加更多细节，关键在于借口（pretext）是否与收件人的工作情境相契合。应据此调整职场网络安全培训策略，帮助员工识别基于真实上下文的个性化钓鱼攻击。

Abstract: Large language models can insert workplace details into phishing pretexts at low cost, but those details may either support or undermine a message's credibility. We recruited 180 U.S. working adults to evaluate simulated, AI-generated phishing emails in a disclosed survey. The emails used four cumulative levels of information: workplace (Level 1); recipient name and job title; job responsibilities; and coworker/shared-project context (Level 4). Participants rated each message's convincingness from 0 to 100, chose one stated action (open the link, investigate, delete, or report), and explained why their highest- and lowest-rated messages stood out. Across 1,436 valid evaluations, convincingness increased by 2.40 points per personalization level in a sensitivity analysis, while the odds of expressing click intention increased by 28\% per level. Among participants who did not express an intention to click, investigation remained common, reporting declined, and deletion increased. A post-hoc descriptive analysis found higher ratings and click intention for messages from a named person who referenced a supplied coworker than for messages from a department or entity. Qualitative coding showed why added detail could help or hurt: details that matched participants' roles and routines supported credibility, while incorrect, vague, or channel-inappropriate details raised suspicion. Together, the results highlight that personalization is not simply a matter of adding more details: it depends on whether the pretext fits the recipient's work context. We discuss how this distinction can inform workplace cybersecurity training.

</details>


### [193] [Nebulon Enterprise Simulated Threats for Phishing Research (NEST-Phish): A Synthetic Enterprise Phishing Email Dataset for Behavioral and Machine-Learning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04474)
*Emily J. Winokur,Lauren S. Treiman,Allen G. Moore,Paul Schutte,Joe Ingram,Danielle N. Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为Nebulon的虚构企业环境下的合成钓鱼邮件数据集，涵盖广泛的工作场所沟通主题，包含配对的合法与钓鱼邮件及可解释的钓鱼线索注释。人类标注分类与分类器评估表明该数据集支持有意义的钓鱼判断差异，并为监督检测提供可学习信号，旨在支持企业场景下的钓鱼检测、人类易感性、可解释性和基准开发研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钓鱼攻击仍是最持久的网络威胁之一，但缺乏可用于研究企业真实邮件环境中钓鱼现象的可公开共享数据集，制约了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 围绕虚构组织Nebulon构建合成企业钓鱼邮件数据集，覆盖多样化职场沟通主题，生成配对的合成合法（非钓鱼类）与钓鱼邮件，并附带可解释的钓鱼线索标注。其中"合法"指非钓鱼类别，而非真实发生的组织邮件。

Result: 人类受试者分类和分类器评估表明，该数据集既支持有意义的钓鱼判断差异，又能为有监督检测提供可学习信号。

Conclusion: 该公开资源旨在支持企业类情境下的钓鱼检测、人类易感性研究、可解释性分析及基准测试开发等未来工作。

Abstract: Phishing remains one of the most persistent cyber threats, yet publicly shareable datasets for studying phishing in realistic enterprise email settings remain limited. To address this gap, we introduce a synthetic enterprise phishing email dataset built around a fictitious organization, Nebulon. The dataset spans a broad set of workplace communication themes and includes matched synthetic legitimate and phishing emails with interpretable phishing-cue annotations. Here, ``legitimate'' denotes the non-phishing class, not legitimately occurring organizational emails. Human-subject categorizations and classifier evaluations show that the dataset supports meaningful variation in phishing judgments while also providing learnable signal for supervised detection. This publicly released resource is intended to support future work on phishing detection, human susceptibility, explainability, and benchmark development in enterprise-like contexts.

</details>


### [194] [Client-Side Probing of Deleted Ridge Statistics in Federated Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04475)
*Yijun Quan,Giovanni Montana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了联邦遗忘系统中的一个隐私与完整性漏洞：系统为支持精确删除而广播更新后的线性分类器，恶意客户端可利用这些广播逆向推导出隐藏的训练特征摘要，从而恢复被删除样本的标签、类别或客户端摘要，并可将其重新插入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦遗忘旨在无需从头重训即可移除客户端数据。为高效实现精确删除，一些系统存储紧凑的可加性训练摘要并在每次变更后广播更新的线性分类器。然而这些广播是否泄露服务器内部状态未被充分研究。本文旨在揭示此类广播可导致隐藏摘要被逆向工程，构成隐私与完整性风险。

Method: 作者首先形式化刻画了观测信息中包含足够独立信息以恢复摘要的确切条件；针对无限制探针给出了匹配的最优构造方案；并基于攻击者自身数据构成的添加操作推导了一个更实际的估计器。通过比较隔离删除前后的服务器状态差异来推断被删除样本的信息。实验在MNIST和CIFAR-10上进行，考察不同广播精度的影响。

Result: 在高精度广播条件下，两种探针类型都能对每个测试样本删除实现精确标签恢复；低精度广播显著降低细粒度恢复能力；响应多样性不足时则完全无法识别。无限制探针因大小特征容易被检测，而大多数单个攻击者数据添加操作与诚实批次相似。 

Conclusion: 研究确定了具体的隐私和完整性风险及其代数成因，并提出了实际的限制因素，包括广播精度、更新验证、响应率和并发活动等防御维度。

Abstract: Federated unlearning aims to remove a client's data from a shared model without retraining from scratch. Some efficient systems make deletion exact by storing compact, additive summaries of the training features and broadcasting an updated linear classifier after every accepted change. We show that these broadcasts can also reveal the hidden summaries. A malicious client can submit known changes, use the returned classifiers to identify the server state, and compare states immediately before and after an isolated deletion. This exposes the deleted sample, class, or client summary and can enable its reinsertion. We characterize exactly when the observations contain enough independent information, give a matching optimal construction for unrestricted probes, and derive a more realistic estimator based on additions formed from the attacker's own data. On MNIST and CIFAR-10, high-precision broadcasts permit exact label recovery for every tested sample deletion with both probe types. Lower-precision broadcasts sharply reduce fine-grained recovery, and insufficiently diverse responses prevent identification altogether. Unrestricted probes are readily detected by their size; most individual attacker-data additions resemble honest batches, although we do not claim that the complete sequence is inconspicuous. The results identify a concrete privacy and integrity risk, its algebraic cause, and practical limits involving broadcast precision, update verification, response rate, and concurrent activity.

</details>


### [195] [Hoss: Fast Oblivious Semantic Search with Heterogeneous GPU-CPU-TEE Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04522)
*Jianzhang Du,Weijie Huang,Chenghong Wang,Nicolas Tsagareli,Yukui Luo,XiaoFeng Wang,Zhongshu Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Hoss是一个首创的具有异构CPU-GPU TEE架构的 oblivious 语义搜索系统。利用现代GPU TEE提供的大规模私有内存(Pmem)来盲化内部访问模式，从而突破传统ORAM的Omega(log N)带宽下界限制。系统将热路径HNSW遍历放在GPU TEE的大Pmem上，仅在访问超出GPU容量的图低层时才调用oblivious原语，并设计了host-access ORAM机制。相比Compass实现最高67倍加速，同时保持高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最先进系统Compass通过优化HNSW图上的ORAM实现了oblivious语义搜索，但即使经过激进优化仍存在巨大开销。由于Compass已移除大部分密码学开销，ORAM访问成为主导成本，而受限于著名的Omega(log N)带宽下界。作者发现传统ORAM开销源于有限私有内存的假设，而现代GPU TEE提供大规模私有内存，能够盲化内部访问模式，从而开辟了新的设计空间。

Method: Hoss采用异构CPU-GPU TEE架构：GPU TEE的大私有内存(Pmem)托管热路径HNSW遍历（图的浅层/高频访问部分），超出GPU容量的图低层则卸载到CPU TEE。系统仅在访问低层时才调用oblivious原语。此外，大Pmem的可用性使Hoss能够设计host-access ORAM机制，突破传统性能约束，并引入多种基于数据依赖的优化，这些在先前设计中无法实现。

Result: 原型实现并对照Compass基准测试，Hoss实现了最高67倍加速，同时保持高召回率，且规模越大加速收益越显著。

Conclusion: 通过利用现代GPU TEE大容量私有内存这一硬件趋势，Hoss重新打开了oblivious语义搜索的设计空间，有效规避了传统ORAM的带宽下界约束，在保持安全性的同时显著提升性能，展示了异构CPU-GPU TEE架构在该领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Semantic search is widely deployed in modern AI systems, but protecting both data contents and access patterns remains challenging. The current state-of-the-art system, Compass, achieves oblivious semantic search by building an optimized ORAM over HNSW graphs. However, even with aggressive optimizations, it still incurs large overheads. Closing this performance gap is fundamentally difficult: Compass has already removed most cryptographic overheads, leaving ORAM accesses as the dominant cost, which are constrained by well-known Omega(log N) bandwidth lower bounds. Our key insight is that traditional ORAM overhead stems from the assumption of limited private memory, whereas modern GPU TEEs provide large private memory (Pmem) that blinds internal access patterns (Hunt et al., NSDI '23). This shift opens a new design space. We therefore propose Hoss, a first-of-its-kind oblivious semantic search system with a heterogeneous CPU-GPU TEE architecture that supports fast, scalable search with low cost of ownership. In Hoss, the GPU TEE's large Pmem hosts the hot-path HNSW traversal, while the lower layers of the graph, if they exceed GPU capacity, are offloaded to CPU TEEs. The system invokes oblivious primitives only when accessing these lower layers. The availability of large Pmem also enables new optimization opportunities. For example, Hoss features a host-access ORAM mechanism that goes beyond traditional performance constraints and incorporates several data-dependent optimizations that are not possible in prior designs. We implement a prototype of Hoss and benchmark it against Compass. Our results show that Hoss achieves up to 67x speedup while maintaining high recall, with larger gains at scale.

</details>


### [196] [Repeat-After-Me: Black-Box Adaptive Visual Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04533)
*Sizhe Chen,Yu-Lin Tsai,Ivan Evtimov,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner,Arman Zharmagambetov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Repeat-After-Me，一种黑盒自适应视觉提示注入攻击，能在商用和开源前沿VLM上实现高成功率攻击（Qwen3.6-27B超过80%，GPT-5.5超过47%），可泄露个人信息或发起恶意工具调用；在真实OpenClaw代理中，未受信任用户可通过最小化的注入图像覆盖TOOLS.md，进而实现远程代码执行和敏感数据外泄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本域的提示注入已可达到近乎完美的攻击成功率，但图像域的视觉提示注入在攻击商用前沿VLM以造成实质性危害方面效果显著不足。原因是实现这类攻击需要生成长且格式合规的目标字符串（如精确且可解析的原生工具调用，含准确的函数名和参数），这对现有方法构成重大挑战。作者旨在弥合这一差距，探索图像域中高成功率的黑盒提示注入攻击。

Method: 作者提出Repeat-After-Me，一种黑盒自适应视觉提示注入攻击方法。该方法针对需要长且格式合规目标字符串的场景进行攻击优化，包括泄露个人可识别信息和发起恶意工具调用两类危害行为。作者在多种开源和商用前沿VLM（含Qwen3.6-27B和GPT-5.5）上进行评估，并在真实OpenClaw Discord代理部署环境中测试了攻击效果，还将该攻击与自适应的文本提示注入进行对比。

Result: 在良性用户提示与注入任务语义无关且未口头授权的现实场景下，该方法在Qwen3.6-27B上ASR超过80%，在GPT-5.5上超过47%。注入在一个代理上优化后迁移到两个商用受害者模型保留43-46%的原始ASR，跨样本迁移性保留64-66%的原始ASR。在真实OpenClaw Discord部署中，未受信任用户可通过最小注入图像覆盖TOOLS.md，实现远程代码执行和秘密数据外泄等敏感行为。该攻击在自适应文本提示注入失效的场景下仍能奏效。

Conclusion: Repeat-After-Me证明了图像域中存在高效的黑盒视觉提示注入攻击，突破了此前视觉注入在危害性攻击上效果不佳的限制。该攻击在商业模型上依然有效且具备一定的跨模型迁移性，并能对真实AI代理系统造成实际安全威胁，提示需要针对此类新型攻击向量开发相应的防御机制。

Abstract: Prompt injection is widely recognized as a major security threat to AI agents that interact with untrusted external data, such as websites, documents, and emails. Prior work has shown that, in the text domain, black-box prompt injection can achieve near-perfect attack success rates (ASRs). In the image domain, however, existing visual prompt injection methods are substantially less effective in attacking frontier commercial VLMs for materially harmful behavior. Achieving such outputs is hard because it requires a long and/or format-compliant target string, such as a precise, parseable native tool call with exact function names and arguments. We present Repeat-After-Me, a black-box adaptive visual prompt injection attack that can reveal personally identifiable information or make malicious tool calls. Across both open-weight and commercial frontier VLMs, including Qwen3.6-27B and GPT-5.5, our method achieves ASRs exceeding 80% and 47%, respectively, under a realistic setting in which the benign user prompt is semantically unrelated to the injected task and does not verbally authorize it. In our evaluation, injections optimized on one surrogate retain 43-46% of the original ASR on two commercial victims, and cross-sample transferability retains 64-66% of the original ASR on those two models. We test our attack in a real-world OpenClaw agent: in a default OpenClaw Discord deployment, an untrusted user can use a minimally injected image to overwrite TOOLS.md, enabling future sensitive behaviors like remote code execution and secret exfiltration. We show our new attack vector works in cases where adaptive textual prompt injection fails. We discuss potential defenses.

</details>


### [197] [Optimizing Credential Blast Radius Through Trust Boundaries and Delegation Under Post-Quantum Authentication Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04566)
*Pauli Taipale,Harri Lainio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个联合优化框架，用于在策略和延迟约束下同时选择信任域划分和凭据派生结构，以限制服务交互系统中颁发者被攻破的影响范围。该框架将服务交互图与凭据派生图通过域分配联系起来，并对凭据爆炸半径进行建模与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将交互服务划分为独立的信任域可以限制颁发者被攻破后造成的影响范围，但这会增加跨信任边界的调用数量。此外，后量子密码替代方案会显著增加受限或损丢路径上的跨域延迟。现有方法通常先确定信任域再设计凭据结构，忽略了二者的耦合关系，导致次优设计。

Method: 作者形式化地将信任域分配与凭据派生结构的选择转化为一个联合优化问题，包含策略与延迟约束。通过域分配将服务交互图和凭据派生图关联起来。凭据爆炸半径被建模为被攻破后服务的加权影响。提出了一个线性上界用于优化求解，并构建了联合事件模型以计算精确的期望影响。对于共享颁发者场景，在凭据覆盖不重叠时线性界是精确的，否则需显式传播。证明了一般问题为NP难，但标量化双域直接颁发情形可归结为加权最小割问题。实验采用穷举合成比较、trace驱动的重放（含实测后量子成本与合成风险输入）以及1-6个信任域的固定派生族进行验证。

Result: 在230个穷举合成比较中，联合优化在195例中比"先界定域再确定凭据结构"的顺序方法取得了更低的爆炸半径，在链式委托场景下尤为明显。trace驱动实验中，在延迟预算内，最优设计相比单域方案将期望影响降低了最多36%。

Conclusion: 该框架将风险假设和实测的跨域成本转化为候选的信任域划分与凭据派生设计方案，为后量子时代服务交互系统的安全架构提供了系统化的优化工具。

Abstract: Partitioning interacting services into independently rooted trust domains limits issuer-compromise reach while increasing calls across trust boundaries. Post-quantum replacements for public-key authentication and key-establishment mechanisms can increase crossing latency on constrained or lossy paths. We formulate the joint selection of trust domains and credential-derivation structures under policy and latency constraints, linking separate service-interaction and credential-derivation graphs through domain assignment. Credential blast radius measures weighted service impact after compromise. A linear upper bound supports optimization, while a joint event model gives exact expected impact. For shared issuers, the bound is exact under nonoverlapping credential reach and otherwise requires explicit propagation. While the general problem is NP-hard, scalarized two-domain direct issuance reduces to a weighted minimum cut. Joint optimization yields lower blast radius than choosing boundaries first in 195 of 230 exhaustive synthetic comparisons, especially under chained delegation. A trace-derived replay used measured post-quantum costs, synthetic risk inputs, a fixed derivation family, and one to six trust domains. The best design found reduced expected impact by up to 36% relative to one domain within the latency budget. The framework turns risk assumptions and measured crossing costs into candidate trust-domain and credential-derivation designs.

</details>


### [198] [Why Is SHAP Not a Reliable Standalone Explanation Framework for Malware Detection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04626)
*Seyedreza Mohseni,Edward Raff,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证SHAP在恶意软件检测解释中的不足：SHAP解释的是分析师选择的一个特定的特征联盟博弈，而非数据中的恶意行为。在PE特征空间中特征间的依赖性会导致条件SHAP稀释特征贡献、归因于模型未使用的特征甚至反转符号，而干预SHAP则查询偏离真实流形的联盟。实验在EMBER和BODMAS数据集上证实了这些效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在恶意软件检测中广泛使用，但其决策需要被解释以满足可解释性要求。分析师需要知道模型学到的是真实的恶意行为还是数据集的特定模式。虽然SHAP被广泛用作标准解释工具并具备理论保证，但这些保证对于可靠的恶意软件解释是不够的。

Method: 作者通过理论证明指出条件SHAP在多特征依赖环境下会产生贡献稀释（因子1/m）、虚假归因和符号反转问题，而干预SHAP则采样偏离流形的不可实现组合。实验中使用EMBER-2018、EMBER-2024和BODMAS数据集，搭配固定的LightGBM和XGBoost检测器来验证这些理论效应。

Result: 实验确认了SHAP在PE特征空间中的解释缺陷：条件SHAP对冗余特征稀释模型的特征信用，将重要性归因于模型实际未使用的特征，并且当数据分布变化时反转未使用特征的归因符号；干预SHAP则产生不存在于真实可执行文件中的离流形联盟。

Conclusion: SHAP应被定位为一种有限的诊断工具，需要明确陈述数据分布和领域验证，而非对恶意软件行为的独立解释。

Abstract: Machine learning is widely used for malware detection, but its decisions must be explained. An analyst needs to know whether a model has learned genuine malicious behavior or only dataset-specific patterns \cite{gaur2021semantics}. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) is the standard tool for this, backed by formal properties such as local accuracy, missingness, and consistency. We argue that these guarantees are insufficient for reliable malware interpretation. We claim SHAP explains a chosen feature-coalition game, not malware behavior in the data. That game is fixed only after the analyst selects the feature players, the missing feature rule, the background distribution, and the simplified input mapping. In static Portable Executable feature spaces, groups such as byte histograms, byte-entropy, strings, headers, sections, imports, and data-directories are not independent signals but are jointly shaped by file structure, packing, compiler behavior, and family conventions. We prove that this dependence makes conditional SHAP dilute a model's feature credit by a factor of $1/m$ across $m-1$ redundant features, attributes importance to features the model never uses, and even reverses the sign of an unused feature's attribution when the data distribution changes; interventional SHAP, meanwhile, queries off-manifold coalitions that no real executable would exhibit. Experiments on EMBER-2018, EMBER-2024, and BODMAS with fixed LightGBM and XGBoost detectors confirm these effects. We therefore position SHAP as a limited diagnostic that requires an explicitly stated data distribution and domain validation, not a standalone account of malware behavior.

</details>


### [199] [Memory-Efficient Designs for Word-Wise Universal Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04769)
*Ardhi Wiratama Baskara Yudha,Erwin Eko Wahyudi,Rian Adam Rajagede,Qian Lou,Yan Solihin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BXT是一个针对全同态加密(FHE)的内存瓶颈优化框架，通过密文压缩、密文序列化、延迟种子生成和密文数字修剪四种技术，在CNN推理任务上相较100倍GPU基线实现了最高3.8倍加速，且精度损失小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE虽然能提供极强的隐私保护，但执行速度远慢于原始计算。随着计算加速技术（如GPU）的进步，性能瓶颈已从计算转移到内存，尤其是FHE将数据规模放大数个数量级导致算术强度（arithmetic intensity）很低，内存带宽成为主要限制因素。

Method: BXT框架通过四种技术缓解内存瓶颈：(1)密文压缩——在执行过程中从种子重新生成密文分量；(2)密文序列化——将系数打包为位数组并在L2到L1传输时解包；(3)延迟种子生成——将依赖PRNG（伪随机数生成器）的离线工作跨聚合操作推迟；(4)密文数字修剪——结合为Universal FHE定制的高容错感知训练进行引导。方法整体利用FHE密文的可再生成性和确定性结构来减少数据在内存层级间的实际传输量。

Result: 在CNN推理任务上，BXT-CSO50配置相比100倍GPU基线实现了最高3.8倍加速，在50%比较精度下精度损失小于1%。

Conclusion: BXT证明了通过重新设计数据在内存中的存在形态和传输方式（而非仅关注计算加速），可以有效解决FHE因数据膨胀导致的低算术强度问题，从而将优化视角从计算密集型转向存储/带宽密集型，为FHE在实际场景中的落地提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables computation on encrypted data, preserving privacy throughout analysis. While its privacy is very strong, FHE is much slower to execute than the original computation. In particular, due to the recent success in accelerating its compute, the performance bottleneck shifts to the memory, especially considering that FHE magnifies the data size by orders of magnitude, resulting in a low arithmetic intensity.
  We propose BXT, an FHE optimization framework that mitigates the memory bottleneck through four techniques: (1) ciphertext compression, which regenerates ciphertext components from seeds during execution; (2) ciphertext serialization, which packs coefficients as bit arrays and unpacks them during L2-to-L1 transfer; (3) delayed seed generation, which defers PRNG-heavy offline work across aggregated operations; and (4) ciphertext digit pruning guided by fault-aware training tailored for Universal FHE. On CNN inference, the BXT-CSO50 configuration effectively achieves up to 3.8$\times$ speedup over the 100x GPU baseline with less than 1% accuracy loss at 50% comparison precision.

</details>


### [200] [Injected and Leaked: Actively Inducing Side-Channel Leakage Using Electromagnetic Injection and Hardware Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04785)
*Haoran Yan,Ziyu Shao,Shuhao Zhang,Qinhong Jiang,Yan Long*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出注入诱导电磁侧信道（Injection-Induced EM Side Channels）框架，通过电磁注入主动放大原本不可行的侧信道泄漏，实现集成闭环EM安全分析。作者设计InjectEave攻击，成功在30米外（含穿墙场景）窃听有线/无线耳机播放的音频，并展示了智能家居功耗、模拟传感器输入等低频秘密的泄漏，以及对固定电话的闭环窃听与操纵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上电磁侧信道泄漏和电磁注入被视为两种截然不同的物理现象，分别威胁数据机密性和完整性。但该工作发现，电磁注入可被用于放大原本在实际场景中不可行（微弱或难以获取）的侧信道泄漏，从而将两种威胁统一起来形成新的安全攻击面。

Method: 作者提出注入诱导EM侧信道的统一分析框架，结合理论建模与实验测量。理论显示非线性硬件组件（如放大器、ADC、电源转换器）可将秘密电信号调制到注入的EM载波上，将低频秘密上变频为可测量的EM辐射。通过调节注入频率与幅度，攻击者可主动塑造泄漏的有效频谱与熵。

Result: 设计并实现了InjectEave攻击，在30米距离（含穿墙场景）使用可获得的RF设备成功窃听有线与无线耳机音频；同时刻画了智能家居设备功耗、模拟传感器输入等其他低频秘密的注入诱导EM泄漏；案例研究展示了固定电话会话的闭环窃听与操纵能力。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地将EM注入与侧信道泄漏集成到统一框架中，揭示了一个此前未被认知的主动攻击范式：攻击者不仅能被动观察泄漏，还能通过注入主动塑造和放大泄漏。文章最后分析了更广泛的安全挑战与可能的缓解措施。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel leakage and injection are typically treated as distinct physical phenomena, threatening data confidentiality and integrity respectively. This work investigates how EM injection can be used to amplify side-channel leakage that is otherwise infeasible. We introduce a novel framework for Injection-Induced EM Side Channels to enable integrated, closed-loop EM security analysis. Our theoretical modeling and experimental measurements reveal that nonlinear hardware components, such as ubiquitous amplifiers, analog-to-digital converters, and power converters, can modulate secret electrical signals onto an injected EM carrier and thus upconvert low-frequency secrets into measurable EM emissions. By tuning the injection frequency and amplitude, adversaries gain the ability to actively shape the effective spectrum and entropy of the resulting leakage. We design InjectEave attack and demonstrate eavesdropping on the audio played through wired and wireless headphones from up to 30 m away with accessible RF equipment, as well as in through-wall scenarios, and characterize injection-induced EM leakage of other low-frequency secrets such as power consumption of smart home devices and analog sensor inputs. Case studies further demonstrate how the proposed techniques enable closed-loop eavesdropping and manipulation of landline-phone conversations. Finally, we analyze the broader security challenges and mitigations.

</details>


### [201] [Cost-Aware Hierarchical Multi-Agent Ransomware Detection and Family Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04820)
*Mubashar Iqbal,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种成本感知的层次化多智能体系统（HMAS），通过分层控制器和元编排器实现自适应勒索软件检测，静态分析作为低成本初始模态，仅在置信度不足或智能体分歧时选择性启用动态与内存模态，实现了96.57%的检测准确率并将平均分析成本降低43.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件检测需融合多种模态（静态、动态、内存）以应对加壳、混淆、进程操纵和运行时逃逸技术，但传统多模态方法对所有样本无条件使用全部模态，导致不必要的计算开销和延迟增加。因此需要在保持准确性的前提下实现自适应、成本感知的模态选择。

Method: 提出HMAS架构：将专门化智能体组织为层次化领域控制器，由元编排器（Meta Orchestrator）协调。以静态分析作为初始低成本模态，当置信度不足或专家智能体出现分歧时选择性调用动态和内存模态。构建成本模型整合模态使用与处理开销，驱动编排策略在性能与计算成本间平衡。引入本地部署的大语言模型对困难样本进行验证，但不替代确定性流水线。

Result: 与静态-only、静态+动态和穷举分析策略对比，完整HMAS在二元检测中达到96.57%准确率、0.96 F1-score和0.99 ROC-AUC；家族归因达到0.90 macro-F1。平均分析成本较穷举分析降低43.97%，延迟显著减少（LLM介入场景除外）。路由分析显示56.05%案例仅凭静态证据即可解决，仅4.33%需要完整证据流水线。

Conclusion: 自适应HMAS能够为勒索软件分析提供准确的成本-性能权衡，同时支持异构和不完整模态，验证了按需调用模态的编排策略的有效性与实际价值。

Abstract: Ransomware detection and family attribution require analysis of different modalities because it can use packing, obfuscation, process manipulation and runtime evasion techniques. However, conventional multimodal usually uses all available modalities for every sample resulting in unnecessary computational cost and increased latency. In this paper, we present a Cost Aware Hierarchical Multi-Agent System (HMAS) for adaptive ransomware detection. The proposed architecture organizes specialized agents into hierarchical domain controllers coordinated by a Meta Orchestrator. Static analysis is used as the initial low-cost modality while additional dynamic and memory modality is selectively used when confidence is insufficient or specialist agents exhibit disagreement. A cost model incorporates modality use and processing overhead. It enables the orchestration policy to balance analysis performance against computational cost. A locally deployed large language model provides verification for selected difficult cases without replacing the deterministic pipeline. Experimental evaluation compares adaptive HMAS with static only, static plus dynamic and exhaustive analysis policies across binary ransomware detection and multiclass family attribution. The complete HMAS achieved 96.57% accuracy, 0.96 F1-score and 0.99 ROC-AUC for binary detection. It also achieved 0.90 macro-F1 for family attribution. At the same time, the HMAS reduced average analysis cost by 43.97% relative to exhaustive analysis and substantially reduced average analysis latency except for the case where LLM is used. Routing analysis showed that 56.05% of cases were resolved using static evidence alone. Only 4.33% required the complete evidence pipeline. These findings demonstrate that adaptive HMAS can provide accuracy cost tradeoff for ransomware analysis while retaining support for heterogeneous and incomplete modalities.

</details>


### [202] [Forgetting Without Restarting: Execution-State Unlearning for Stateful LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04875)
*Chao Yao,Yangbo Wei,Zhen Huang,Junhong Qian,Chenle Chen,Shaoqiang Lu,Chen Wu,Lei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"执行状态遗忘"（execution-state unlearning）概念，面向长时运行的LLM智能体，其状态不仅包含对话记录，还包括压缩摘要、明文记忆、待执行工具计划及KV缓存。核心贡献是证明精确遗忘的下界（至少需T-τ+1次重算转换），并提出"溯源引导的选择性重放"（Provenance-Guided Selective Replay）方法，通过跨层协奏（提示、压缩记忆、缓存）实现这一最优界，即在多个智能体套件、基线及模型族上，该方法与完全重置在行为上不可区分，且减少了最多9倍的重算token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的"遗忘"操作仅删除明文记忆记录即止步，而由被撤销信息所衍生的各类工件（摘要、计划、KV缓存）仍然保持完整，导致被遗忘的信息仍可通过这些派生状态泄露。该问题在理论形式化与高效方法两方面均未被充分解决。

Method: 本文首先将智能体运行时建模为确定性转移系统，从理论上刻画遗忘问题的结构：证明目标前轨迹前缀可免费共享于反事实世界；目标后后缀若无token级归因则不可约地被污染；精确遗忘至少需要T-τ+1次重算转换。随后提出Provenance-Guided Selective Replay方法，通过溯源图定位注入点，将检查点恢复化为KV缓存裁剪，并以净化后的重放再生反事实后缀，从而跨提示、压缩记忆与缓存三层实现协同遗忘。

Result: 在三个智能体套件、九个基线与三个模型族上，使用言语诱发（elicitation）、随机与无字符串行为测试进行审计表明：单纯删除记忆不改变泄露；基于指令的遗忘在诱发测试下完全失效（Leak@probes=1.00）；来源删改（source redaction）在80%的剧集中仍作用于被撤销的偏好；而选择性重放与完全重置在行为上不可区分，且所需重算的token数量减少了最多9倍。

Conclusion: 遗忘必须以执行状态（含压缩记忆与KV缓存）为粒度进行跨层协同处理，而非仅删除明文记录。Provenance-Guided Selective Replay首次在理论最优重算界内实现有效的执行状态遗忘，为LLM智能体的合规遗忘提供了可证明且高效的系统化方案。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents are stateful: beyond the transcript they accrete compressed summaries, plaintext memory, pending tool plans, and, under every serving API, a KV cache. Yet today's "forget" operations delete a plaintext memory record and stop, leaving every artifact derived from the revoked information intact. We formalize execution-state unlearning: after a forget request, the agent must behave as if it had never observed the target. Modeling the runtime as a deterministic transition system, we prove that the pre-target trajectory prefix is shared with this counterfactual world for free, that the post-target suffix is irreducibly tainted without token-level attribution, and that exact unlearning requires at least $T-τ+1$ recomputed transitions, where $τ$ is the target's injection step. Provenance-Guided Selective Replay attains this bound as a cross-layer contract spanning prompt, compressed memory, and cache: a provenance graph locates the injection point, checkpoint restoration reduces to cropping the KV cache, and sanitized replay regenerates the counterfactual suffix. Audited with elicitation, stochastic, and string-free behavioral tests across three agent suites, nine baselines, and three model families, memory deletion leaves leakage unchanged, instruction-based forgetting collapses under elicitation (Leak@probes = 1.00), and source redaction still acts on a revoked preference in 80% of episodes, while selective replay is indistinguishable from a full reset at up to 9x fewer recomputed tokens.

</details>


### [203] [ReCAST: Restoration-aware Cascaded Stage-wise Training for Obfuscated SMS Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04878)
*Jieyun Huang,Yi Shen,Kaikai Zhao,Jiangze Yan,Wenjing Zhang,Ping Chen,Ning Wang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ReCAST框架，通过恢复感知的级联阶段式训练，将大教师模型的去混淆能力蒸馏到小型可部署学生模型中，以提升在混淆中文短信场景下的风险分类鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文短信中的欺诈消息越来越多地使用精心设计的混淆策略来隐藏风险短语，同时保持人类可读性。生产系统中的低成本分类器在真实延迟和吞吐量约束下对这类混淆文本的直接分类非常脆弱，需要一种能同时兼顾鲁棒性和部署可行性的解决方案。

Method: 提出ReCAST（Restoration-aware Cascaded Stage-wise Training）框架，采用级联阶段式训练：从大型教师模型中蒸馏去混淆能力到小型学生模型，蒸馏过程监督三个子任务——混淆片段检测、混淆类型预测和文本恢复；随后利用恢复感知的学生模型进行下游风险分类。

Result: 在内部构建的真实世界中文短信基准上，ReCAST相比直接在混淆文本上训练的基线模型，在分类性能上取得了显著提升，验证了恢复感知蒸馏方法在混淆短信分类中的有效性。

Conclusion: 恢复感知的蒸馏方法为在生产约束下使用更小的可部署模型实现鲁棒短信风险分类提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Fraudulent messages sent via Short Message Service (SMS) are increasingly obfuscated to evade cost-conscious classifiers in production systems. In Chinese SMS, attackers can exploit a wide range of carefully crafted obfuscation strategies to hide risk-bearing phrases while preserving human readability, making direct classification brittle under real-world latency and throughput constraints. We propose ReCAST, a Restoration-aware Cascaded Stage-wise Training framework for robust obfuscated Chinese SMS classification. ReCAST distills a large teacher model's de-obfuscation ability into a smaller deployable student model by supervising obfuscated span detection, obfuscation type prediction, and text restoration, and then uses the restoration-aware student for downstream risk classification. Experiments on an internally constructed real-world Chinese SMS benchmark show that ReCAST substantially improves classification performance over directly trained baselines under obfuscation. The results suggest that restoration-aware distillation offers a practical path toward robust SMS risk classification with smaller deployable models under production-oriented constraints.

</details>


### [204] [The Security Feature Location Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04899)
*Kevin Hermann,Sven Peldszus,Thorsten Berger,Adam Shostack*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"安全特征定位"这一任务，旨在将代码位置与安全特征（如认证、加密）关联起来，帮助开发者理解安全实现并评估系统是否真正实施了所期望的安全属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件安全需要通过具体的认证、加密等安全特征来实现，但开发者难以确定一个系统实现了哪些安全特征以及这些特征具体在代码的哪个位置实现。缺乏这种可追溯性使得开发者难以理解安全实现，也无法评估系统是否真正落实了预期的安全属性。

Method: 本文正式定义了"安全特征定位"任务，即建立代码位置与安全特征之间的映射关系，使开发者能追溯安全功能的实现位置。

Result: 提出了安全特征定位这一新任务框架，为开发者理解安全实现和评估安全属性是否被强制执行提供了基础。

Conclusion: 通过将安全特征与代码位置关联，使开发者能够理解系统安全实现的全貌，并验证预期的安全属性是否真正落实，从而支撑软件安全审计与评估。

Abstract: Software security must be realized through security features such as authentication and encryption, but which features does a system implement, and where? We present security feature location: the task of relating code locations to security features, enabling developers to understand security implementations and assess whether intended security properties are enforced.

</details>


### [205] [Robust Coverless Linguistic Steganography via Sentence Embedding Space with Global Resynchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04970)
*Lizhi Xiong,Yuping Lu,Jun Li,Ziqiang Li,Zhangjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于句子嵌入空间的鲁棒无载体隐写框架，通过层次聚类路径编码秘密消息，并引入全局重同步机制（GRM）解决比特滑移问题，在词级和句级扰动下显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言隐写方法主要依赖token级操作，在词级和句级文本扰动下可靠性不足；同时可变长编码方案在轻微扰动下极易发生比特滑移，导致嵌入与提取的比特序列失同步，传统方法难以同时保证鲁棒性和隐蔽性。

Method: 1) 构建无载体隐写框架，在句子嵌入空间而非token空间操作；2) 将秘密消息编码为句子嵌入空间中的层次聚类路径，增强对词级和句级扰动的解码稳定性；3) 提出全局重同步机制（GRM），将可变长比特流重构为锚定在语义子空间上的离散符号，将局部嵌入失败与全局消息恢复解耦，解决比特滑移问题。

Result: 在词级和句级扰动条件下，该方法相比现有方法实现了显著的鲁棒性提升，同时保持有效的嵌入容量，并展现出对统计分析较强的抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 句子嵌入空间的层次聚类路径编码结合GRM机制，能够有效克服文本扰动导致的解码不稳定和比特滑移问题，为鲁棒语言隐写提供了新思路。

Abstract: Linguistic steganography enables covert communication through natural language. Existing methods heavily rely on token-level operations and struggle to maintain reliability under word- and sentence-level textual perturbations. Moreover, variable-length coding-based schemes are highly susceptible to bit-slippage under minor disturbances, as perturbations cause desynchronization between embedded and extracted bit sequences. To address these issues, we propose a robust coverless steganographic framework that operates in the sentence embedding space rather than the token space. Specifically, secret messages are encoded as hierarchical clustering paths in the sentence embedding space, which enhances decoding stability against word- and sentence-level textual perturbations. To tackle the bit-slippage problem, we introduce a Global Resynchronization Mechanism (GRM) that reframes variable-length bitstreams as discrete symbols anchored to semantic subspaces, decoupling local embedding failures from global message recovery. Experimental results demonstrate that under word- and sentence-level perturbations, our approach achieves substantial improvements in robustness, while maintaining effective embedding capacity and exhibiting strong resistance to statistical analysis.

</details>


### [206] [TPMSpy: Validation of Measured Boot Systems by Low-Level Tracing of TPM Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05011)
*Roman Lacko,Petr Svenda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种平台无关的方法，在虚拟化系统与TPM交互层面分析低层TPM使用，独立重建并验证TPM事件日志而不依赖引证机制。该方法适用于开放和封闭系统，并在Linux和Windows上验证，同时对Linux系统中systemd版本245-258（2020-2025）的Measured Boot使用进行了纵向分析，发现广泛差异、未记录的变更行为以及用户空间systemd服务测量不一致等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Measured Boot机制虽已被当代操作系统广泛部署，但在实践中其安全性取决于实现是否正确测量了预期组件，而这种测量未被系统性检查。现有方法缺乏对TPM实际使用情况的独立验证手段，且常依赖引证（quoting）机制本身。

Method: 提出一种平台无关的分析方法，在虚拟化系统与TPM交互的层面分析低层TPM使用，独立重建和验证TPM事件日志，不依赖引证机制且不依赖具体实现细节，因此适用于开放和封闭系统。在Linux和Windows上部署演示，并对Linux系统systemd 245-258版本进行系统化纵向分析。

Result: 方法在Linux和Windows上均得到验证。纵向分析发现Measured Boot使用方式在系统间呈现广泛分化，无单一使用模式出现；分析识别出未记录的（行为）变更，并揭示用户空间systemd服务的测量不一致，导致远程引证不可靠，且在这些系统上LUKS磁盘解密存在问题。

Conclusion: Measured Boot的实践安全性与系统实际测量行为密切相关，但当前实现存在显著差异和缺陷。平台无关的独立TPM事件日志验证方法是必要的分析工具，可用于发现实现层面的不一致性和未记录行为，从而保障远程引证和依赖Measured Boot的安全功能的可靠性。

Abstract: Measured Boot extends trust in a booted system by recording cryptographic measurements of executed software and system state into a Trusted Platform Module (TPM), enabling subsequent verification through remote attestation. Although this mechanism is increasingly deployed in contemporary operating systems, its practical security depends on whether implementations measure the expected components under the expected conditions, yet this is not checked systematically.
  We propose a platform-agnostic method for analysing low-level TPM usage at the level of virtualized system--TPM interactions. It enables independent reconstruction and validation of the TPM Event Log without relying on the quoting mechanism itself. Because it does not depend on implementation details, it is applicable to both open and closed systems. We demonstrate the method on both Linux and Windows and conduct a systematic longitudinal analysis of Linux systems with systemd versions 245--258 (2020--2025), examining how Measured Boot usage evolved and observing wide divergence. No single usage pattern emerged amongst systems, warranting customized analysis.
  The analysis identifies undocumented behavioural changes, reveals inconsistent measurements of user-space systemd services, which prevent reliable remote attestation and LUKS disk decryption on such systems.

</details>


### [207] [Has MIMO decoding been proved hard from lattice problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05013)
*Yang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Dean和Goldsmith提出的将格问题归约到MIMO解码的多项式时间归约进行了理论审查，指出该归约在非模MIMO场景下无法延续LWE归约的结构特性，因此其已发表的证明并不能确立所声称的MIMO解码计算硬度。作者区分了硬度证明中的缺陷与针对特定参数的直接攻击，并阐明了任何试图修复该归约所需满足的条件，但不排除MIMO物理层安全在总体上成立的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作虽然提出了针对该构造的攻击和反例，引发了对MIMO物理层安全性的质疑，但对其归约方法的精确有效性和局限性的理解仍不完整。作者旨在从理论上系统检验该修正归约，厘清其证明失效的根本原因。

Method: 通过理论分析，对修订后的归约进行严格考察，识别出LWE归约中那些无法迁移到非模（non-modular）MIMO场景的结构特征，从而论证其发表的硬度证明不能推导出MIMO解码的声称硬度。

Result: 发现该归约的已发布证明并未建立所声称的MIMO解码的格点硬度保证，其失效源于LWE归约的结构特征在非模MIMO环境下无法成立；同时厘清了硬度证明中的缺陷与针对特定参数攻击之间的区别。

Conclusion: MIMO物理层安全系统的格硬度保证不能从现有的归约得以确立，但本文并未排除MIMO系统物理层安全在整体上的可行性，仅指明现有归约路径不足以提供计算安全的理论基础。

Abstract: Multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technology is fundamental to modern wireless communication. Physical layer security seeks to protect transmitted information by exploiting properties of the noisy communication channel. Dean and Goldsmith proposed a polynomial time reduction from lattice problems to MIMO decoding by adapting Regev's reduction for learning with errors (LWE). If valid, this reduction would give physical layer security a strong computational foundation based on the hardness of established lattice problems. Subsequent works presented attacks and counterexamples against the resulting construction, casting doubt on its security but leaving the precise validity and limitations of the underlying reduction incompletely understood. We provide a theoretical examination of the revised reduction and identify the structural features of the LWE reduction that fail to carry over to the non-modular MIMO setting, hence showing that its published proof does not establish the claimed hardness of MIMO decoding. Our results distinguish flaws in the hardness proof from direct attacks on particular parameter choices and clarify what would be required of any attempted repair. We do not rule out physical layer security for MIMO systems in general, but show that the claimed lattice hardness guarantee does not follow from the existing reduction.

</details>


### [208] [Operational Roles of QRNG-Derived Quantum Entropy in Bitcoin Proof-of-Work Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05092)
*Ricardo Fernandes da Silva,Paulo Vitor Batista Santos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的基准框架，通过两个调度层可观测指标（熵效率因子η和重启多样性指数ρ）来衡量混合量子-经典挖矿基础设施中量子熵的操作价值。模拟显示，在诚实的比特币PoW场景下（候选区块头保持不同），QRNG并不会改变成功概率，其价值主要体现在涉及相关重启故障、命名空间复用和熵来源保障等保障导向场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注器件层面的QRNG演示，缺乏从运营/调度层面系统评估量子熵在混合挖矿基础设施中实际操作价值的可复现基准方法。

Method: 提出两个调度层可观测指标（熵效率因子η与重启多样性指数ρ），通过Monte Carlo模拟与调度器模拟（带置信区间），对比确定性基线、强经典随机基线与QRNG在多种故障场景下的表现。

Result: 在受试的确定性与强经典基线上，QRNG在挖矿成功概率上显示平价；QRNG的价值在涉及相关重启故障、命名空间复用与熵来源保障等保障导向场景中出现。

Conclusion: 该研究定位为基于模拟的验证框架而非器件级QRNG演示，下一步实验方向是引入录制或实时QRNG流的硬件在环验证。

Abstract: Replacing classical entropy with QRNG output does not change honest Bitcoin PoW success probability when candidate headers remain distinct. The original contribution of this paper is a reproducible benchmark that locates and measures the operational value of quantum entropy in hybrid quantum-classical mining infrastructure through two scheduler-level observables, the entropy-efficiency factor $η$ and the reboot-diversity index $ρ$. Monte Carlo and scheduler simulations with confidence intervals show parity for competent deterministic and strong-classical baselines, while QRNG value emerges in assurance-oriented scenarios involving correlated restart faults, namespace reuse, and entropy provenance. The study is therefore positioned as a simulation-based validation framework rather than as a device-level QRNG demonstration; hardware-in-the-loop validation with recorded or live QRNG streams is identified as the next experimental step.

</details>


### [209] [TIER: Threat Implicitness Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Safety Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05117)
*Thu-Hien Trinh-Thi,Hai-Yen Vong,Thanh-Ha Ung-Dung,Tram Ho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TIER（威胁隐含度基准），用于对LLM在具有不同威胁隐含程度的提示下的行为安全进行评估。TIER覆盖四个风险域和四个威胁等级，使用六标签行为量表和两个独立LLM裁判进行评估。实验表明LLM安全行为随威胁等级渐变而非二元切换，并揭示行为感知评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全基准大多依赖二元指标（如是否拒绝或是否越狱成功），忽略了模型在面对不同威胁隐含程度的有害提示时如何反应这一关键维度。需要更细粒度、行为层面的评测框架来准确刻画模型安全行为。

Method: 1) 构建TIER基准，涵盖四个风险域（如危害内容等）和四个威胁等级（从显式有害请求到复杂越狱）；2) 设计六标签行为量表对模型响应进行分类；3) 采用两个独立的LLM裁判进行自动评估；4) 在六个开源LLM上进行实验。

Result: 实验发现：模型安全行为随威胁等级渐变而非从拒绝到顺从二元切换；上下文语境提示产生最多样的行为；越狱暴露最大鲁棒性差距；攻击成功率相似的模型可能呈现完全不同的响应分布。

Conclusion: LLM安全评测需要从二元指标转向行为感知的细粒度评估，TIER提供了一种新范式，能更全面地理解模型安全能力的渐变性和差异性。

Abstract: Current LLM safety benchmarks largely rely on binary metrics, overlooking how models respond to harmful prompts with varying threat implicitness. We introduce TIER, a Threat Implicitness Benchmark for behavioral safety evaluation of LLMs. TIER covers four risk domains and four threat levels, from explicit harmful requests to sophisticated jailbreaks. Responses are assessed using a six-label behavior scale and two independent LLM judges. Experiments on six open-weight LLMs show that safety behaviors evolve gradually across threat levels rather than shifting directly from refusal to compliance. Contextual prompts yield the most diverse behaviors, while jailbreaks reveal the largest robustness gaps. Furthermore, models with similar Attack Success Rates can exhibit distinct response distributions, highlighting the need for behavior-aware LLM safety evaluation.

</details>


### [210] [Understanding the Privacy-Preserving Potential of HTTP/2 Against Webpage Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05119)
*Bogdan Cebere,Prateek Kumar,Sylvain Chatel,Wouter Lueks,Christian Rossow*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究HTTP/2应用层上的网站指纹（WF）防御，展示了如何通过HTTP/2特性在客户端和服务端模拟多种已知防御方法，并利用HTTP/2特有功能（如资源预推、多路复用、流控）挖掘轻量高效的新型防御潜力。作者提出统一评估框架，结合实用攻击、信息论泄漏估计和开销测量，对每种防御识别最强超参数调优的指纹识别模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的WF防御（如噪声、填充、延迟、流拆分）多在Tor或VPN等封装协议层面研究，而忽略了最广泛部署的应用层协议HTTP/2。本文填补了在HTTP/2应用层实施WF防御研究的空白。

Method: 1) 在HTTP/2客户端侧和服务端侧通过协议特性模拟已知防御（HTTPOS、LLaMA、FRONT、Tamaraw、ALPaCA）；2) 利用HTTP/2的被动资源建议、多路复用和流控等特性设计新型防御；3) 构建统一评估蓝图，按数据集校准防御参数，结合实用攻击、两种信息论泄漏估计器和开销测量进行综合评估。

Result: 框架能够为每种防御识别出最强超参数调优的指纹识别模型，并用两个信息论泄漏估计器量化防御引入的残余不确定性，同时考虑隐私与开销的权衡。

Conclusion: HTTP/2的应用层特性为WF防御提供了新的可行途径，客户端和服务端均可部署轻量防御，且统一的评估框架可用于系统比较不同的防御方案。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer which webpage a user visits from encrypted HTTPS traffic alone, compromising privacy even without decryption. WF defenses commonly shape traffic through noise, padding, delays, or flow splitting, yet they are most often studied from the perspective of encapsulating protocols like Tor or VPN rather than at the application layer (HTTP).
  In this work, we focus on application-layer defenses enabled by the most widely deployed version of HTTP, HTTP/2. We demonstrate how known defenses can be emulated through HTTP/2 features at the client side (HTTPOS, LLaMA, FRONT, Tamaraw) and the server side (ALPaCA, Tamaraw). We further show that HTTP/2 features, such as proactive resource suggestion, multiplexing, and flow control, offer untapped potential for lightweight yet effective defenses deployable at both endpoints.
  We evaluate these defenses using a unified blueprint that calibrates defense parameters per dataset, then combines practical attacks, information-theoretic leakage estimates, and overhead measurements. For each defense, this framework identifies the strongest hyperparameter-tuned fingerprinting model and estimates the residual uncertainty induced by the defense using two information-theoretic leakage estimators, all while accounting for the defense's privacy-overhead trade-offs.

</details>


### [211] [Conformal Prediction for Offensive Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05165)
*Giovanni Cherubin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了共形预测（Conformal Prediction, CP）在网络安全领域的应用空白，指出CP虽已诞生超过25年，但在网络攻击（防御性安全）方面的应用极为罕见。作者在隐私保护机器学习和网络流量分析两个攻击性安全关键领域展示了初步研究发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测作为一种具有理论保证的不确定性量化方法，已在防御性安全中被广泛采用，但在进攻性安全（即攻击）领域的应用几乎无法在文献中找到。作者旨在探索这一空白，挖掘CP在攻击场景中的潜在价值。

Method: 作者聚焦于进攻性安全的两大关键领域开展初步研究：(1) 隐私保护机器学习——利用CP的特性进行隐私推理或规避；(2) 网络流量分析——将CP应用于攻击场景中的流量分类与识别。通过试点实验展示CP在上述领域中的攻击性用途。

Result: 研究呈现了CP在隐私保护机器学习和网络流量分析两个进攻性安全方向上的初步发现，填补了CP在攻击性安全应用方面的文献空白，证明了CP不仅可用于防御，也可被用作攻击工具。

Conclusion: CP在进攻性安全领域具有未被充分发掘的潜力，值得在隐私保护机器学习和网络流量分析等方向深入探索其攻击性应用，以促进对该技术双重用途的更全面理解。

Abstract: Despite its introduction more than a quarter century ago, Conformal Prediction (CP) has seen surprisingly few applications to the cyber security world thus far. In particular, we observe that, while CP has been employed as a defensive measure in many recent works, its use for carrying out attacks (i.e., for offensive security) is hard to trace in the literature. We explore this gap, by presenting initial findings in two key areas of offensive security: Privacy-Preserving Machine Learning, and network traffic analysis.

</details>


### [212] [Governing Bring Your Own AI: A Parameterized Maturity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05236)
*Dare Bello,John Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对员工在个人账号下使用自带生成式AI工具（BYOAI，即一种Shadow AI形式）带来的治理缺口，通过系统文献综述提出风险分类和框架参与概况，并开发了一个参数化治理成熟度模型，将五级成熟度阶梯与技术控制架构耦合以评估残余风险。研究基于30份文献，发现数据暴露与合规是最突出的风险类别，且分级控制型方案在参数化条件下能显著降低模型化外泄风险并提高可执行覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 员工越来越多地使用个人拥有的生成式AI工具（如ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude）进行日常工作，这种BYOAI实践发生在企业身份与安全控制之外。现有治理框架仅面向组织管理的AI工具，无法覆盖使用个人账户的非受管AI工具，存在明显的治理空白。

Method: 通过系统性文献综述生成风险分类与框架参与概况，随后开发一个参数化治理模型，度量治理成熟度水平对残余风险的降低程度。该模型将五级成熟度阶梯通过"控制层覆盖率影响安全结果"的链条耦合到技术控制架构。语料库由30份记录（24项研究、6份框架文档）组成。

Result: 最突出的风险类别为数据暴露与合规；框架参与情况不一致。模型结果表明：禁止型解决方案的残余风险水平接近基线方案；而在指定参数化下，分层控制型解决方案能显著降低模型化外泄风险并提高可强制执行的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 研究确立了技术、治理、人力三个相互支撑的支柱以支持安全防护措施，并表明单纯禁止性政策效果有限，分层控制型解决方案在降低BYOAI相关风险方面更为有效。

Abstract: Employees are increasingly using personally owned generative AI tools such as ChatGPT, Gemini, and Claude for their daily work. This practice is known as Bring Your Own AI (BYOAI), which is a distinct form of Shadow AI in which employee-authenticated personal accounts are used outside of enterprise identity and security controls. Existing frameworks were designed for AI tools managed by organizations, and their coverage does not extend to unmanaged AI tools used with a personal account. In addressing these issues, we developed a governance model through a systematic review of the literature that produces a risk taxonomy and a framework-engagement profile. We also developed a parameterized governance model that measures how much a level of governance maturity reduces residual risk. A five-level maturity ladder is coupled to a technical control architecture through a chain in which the coverage of the control layer influences the security outcomes. Our study of a curated corpus of 30 records (24 research studies and 6 framework documents) indicated that the most prominent categories identified were data exposure and compliance, and framework engagement was inconsistent. Three mutually supporting pillars (technical, governance, and human) were established to support safeguards. Additionally, the results of the model demonstrated that prohibition-based solutions will result in residual risk levels close to those achieved through baseline solutions. Under the specified parameterization, layered control-based solutions substantially reduce modeled exfiltration risk and increase enforceable coverage.

</details>


### [213] [CONTINUITY: Security-Context Contracts for Composable LLM Agent Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05269)
*Chris Zheng,Geng Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CONTINUITY框架，解决LLM代理系统中安全机制组合后产生"安全上下文不连续性"的问题。该框架通过assume-guarantee契约对每个组件建模，利用签名根授权、来源承诺、角色绑定转换收据等机制携带认证安全上下文，实现端到端后果完整性。实验在2560个参数化攻击实例中实现零有害外部效果，同时完成全部良性任务和升级所有歧义案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理系统越来越多地集成了来源追踪、授权、策略执行、协议适配器和执行控制等安全机制，但单个正确的安全机制在组合时并不能保证端到端安全——安全关键上下文可能在组件边界处被丢弃、扩大、重绑定或被重新解释。这种"安全上下文不连续性"是此前未被系统识别的失败模式。

Method: CONTINUITY采用两个核心手段：(1) 对每个组件用assume-guarantee契约建模，并使用签名根授权(signed root grants)、来源承诺(provenance commitments)、角色绑定转换收据(role-bound transition receipts)、受限类型释放(bounded typed releases)、转换见证(transformation witnesses)和效果受限执行许可(effect-bound execution permits)等机制携带认证安全上下文；(2) 形式化端到端"后果完整性"(consequence integrity)要求，即每个实现的外部效果必须有有效且当前的授权见证，将委托人、任务、来源、委派、策略状态、规范动作和最终性边界关联起来。实现上提供参考验证器和跨层故障注入套件，覆盖4个应用域中的32类故障。

Result: 在跨越128个故障域类别的2560个参数化攻击实例中，完整的CONTINUITY配置未产生任何有害外部效果，同时完成了全部700个良性任务并升级了所有200个歧义案例，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 安全的代理执行不仅需要各个控制机制自身正确，还需要明确的契约来保持这些保证贯穿从指令到效果的完整路径。CONTINUITY证明了通过显式的组件间契约可以弥合安全上下文不连续性，实现可验证的端到端安全组合。

Abstract: LLM agent systems increasingly combine provenance tracking, authorization, policy enforcement, protocol adapters, and execution controls. However, individually correct security mechanisms do not necessarily compose into an end-to-end secure system: security-critical context may be dropped, widened, rebound, or reinterpreted as actions cross component boundaries. We identify this failure mode as security-context discontinuity and introduce CONTINUITY, a framework for verifiable composition of agent security controls.
  CONTINUITY models each component with an assume-guarantee contract and carries authenticated security context across transitions using signed root grants, provenance commitments, role-bound transition receipts, bounded typed releases, transformation witnesses, and effect-bound execution permits. We formalize end-to-end consequence integrity, requiring every realized external effect to be backed by a valid and current authorization witness linking the principal, task, provenance, delegation, policy state, canonical action, and finality boundary.
  We implement a reference verifier and deterministic cross-layer fault-injection suite covering 32 fault classes across four application domains. In 2,560 parameterized attack instances spanning 128 fault-domain classes, the full CONTINUITY configuration commits no harmful external effect, while completing all 700 benign tasks and escalating all 200 ambiguous cases. These results show that secure agent execution requires not only sound individual controls, but explicit contracts that preserve their guarantees across the complete instruction-to-effect path.

</details>


### [214] [Machine Unlearning as Private Retroactive Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05329)
*Haim Kaplan,Refael Kohen,Yishay Mansour,Kobbi Nissim,Uri Stemmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"私有回溯算法"的概念，将回溯性与持续观察下的差分隐私相结合，在近乎零额外渐近代价下为线性统计、聚类和直方图构造满足双重需求的算法，同时给出不可能性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器反学习（machine unlearning）通常模拟从头重训练的效果，但近期研究表明，这种模拟要求在对序列发布进行观测的敌手面前不具备有意义的隐私语义。这使得反学习不是隐私问题而是数据维护问题，对应到回溯算法。然而，现有的回溯算法缺少隐私保护维度，因此需要将回溯性与差分隐私结合。

Method: 定义私有回溯算法的形式化框架，将回溯性要求与持续观察下的差分隐私（differential privacy under continual observation）结合起来，并针对线性统计、聚类和直方图分别设计相应构造方案，同时给出不可能性结果的证明。

Result: 对于线性统计、聚类和直方图等任务，实现了隐私与回溯性的同时满足，且渐近代价与仅需隐私时相同；此外对某些情形证明了不可能性结果。

Conclusion: 私有回溯算法能够有效弥合机器反学习与差分隐私之间的概念鸿沟，将反学习定位为数据维护问题而非隐私问题，并证明了在多项核心任务上可以同时兼顾隐私与回溯性而无额外渐近开销。

Abstract: Machine unlearning typically aims to emulate retraining from scratch: upon a deletion request, the unlearning algorithm should produce an outcome that would have been obtained had the deleted point never been included. Recent work has shown that this emulation requirement carries no meaningful privacy semantics against an adversary who observes a sequence of releases.
